- B7 / N23 (spec R8 :92, :93, :97, :135, :155). FC01 adds the 'governor' event on applyNativeLoadDecision (decision null, authority null, spent [], completion null, else RECORD_INVALID); it replays step 6's ONE internal governor() (now shared with earn()) for every lift with a native original opener and replaces only holdFlag ('applied' if one changed, else 'unchanged'; effect null). FC03 calls it once per projection after the effects, seeded by the immutable base (no transition writes holdFlag). No export added; both runtimes forward it (byte-equal in R4-N23).

Green: holdFlag true, card "2 reps in reserve" through the real registrar and capture; C3 honest opener and unknown C2 give 'unchanged'. Replay is idempotent on its own output (a set/release depends only on the last two openers), so a check on the projected state prices the same.

An earn keeps its Q (never captured, never landed); the spend is kept; other lifts project. The check lets only that spend's compensation through; its yes retires Q and writes no w. ## Round 6 (UNCOMMITTED on c788f61; Astra L4 REJECT B14-B17; one root: a retire-only undo left no trace)
Red on c788f61 product bytes: FC12 66/72 (6 red), FA03 17/20 (3 red; R6-B17 passes there by design, it is M14's witness) (r6\red-fc12.txt 2D5785EF, r6\red-fa03.txt 4C6B91C4). Green: FC12 72/72, FA03 20/20; with native-trend-context 131 tests, 130 pass, 1 = the EXPOSED pin (r6\final-green.txt 710CB39D). Every earlier counterexample row (B1-B13, D10, D11) is in these suites and green.
- Design (R8 :121 "permanently cancels the targeted effect", :153, :156): the durable tombstone is the compensation's own spend, which the fold rebuilds from its response into the effect frontier on every projection and revision. FC01 refuses COMPENSATION_DESCENDANTS when the frontier already holds ['native-load-compensation', lift, spend], whatever trace the state keeps. A later base can no longer revoke it: the compensation of an effect HELD under an unprovable order is validated at its original cut for ELIGIBILITY for the same effect (retire-only, :156 "the prior image is no longer provably current") instead of body equality, and writes nothing. Two recorded compensations of the same effect are one cancellation: the later body folds unchanged, its ref retained.
- B14: red R6-B14a/b (fold, observed and baseline) and FA03 R6-B14 'offer'. Green: refused, no offer; a second recorded undo with another body folds unchanged under R1 and R2, tombstone kept, no conflict, not re-quarantined.
- B15: red R6-B15a/b (held undo, later base) and R6-B15c (applied adoption undone, later base 102.5) EFFECT_CONFLICT (+RECORD_INVALID); FA03 R6-B15 (close/reopen, base 50) EFFECT_CONFLICT and RECORD_INVALID. Green, R1 and R2: COMPENSATED, no conflict, no RECORD_INVALID, the later base stands.
- B16: red R6-B16 (fold) and FA03 R6-B16 'offer' for a held adoption whose target 60/105 a Start already captured. The check now reads the target from its retained issuance and calls the fold's own capturedAfter predicate. Green: COMPENSATION_DESCENDANTS at check, before any write; the fold refuses a recorded one.
- B17 / M14: FA03 R6-B17 drives the real respond path with a TEST SEAM (no product hook): the shared FC03 module object's checkNativeLoad returns a substituted body and its exported proposalDigest is made to collide with the held id. sameIssued refuses STALE_OFFER with zero writes; M14 (today-bindings back to the digest compare) acknowledges it and is KILLED.
- Mutants (r6\mutants-summary.txt C8160EAE): 32 run, 32 KILLED, including M14 (R6-B17) and the new R6-tombstone, R6-alt, R6-retire-only, R6-check-issuance (FC12 and FA03 variants).
- Full local set (80 files): c788f61 1135/68 fail, this tree 1135/67; the only difference is the base-only "the checkout this suite started on is the checkout it leaves" (r6\full-base.txt B71CDABE, r6\full-head.txt 9C3A8F07).
- For the PM (not a STOP; both follow from the ordered B14/B15 behaviour): (1) two compensations of one effect with different bodies are coalesced, not EFFECT_CONFLICT (reading :121/:156: the same semantic cancellation); (2) an applied adoption undone at its own base, whose base later moves without an ordering op, now folds as a retire-only cancellation (w = the later base), not a restore of the older prior image.

- B18 (R8 :121, :153-156). A compensation's validity no longer re-prices its recorded target: it is valid when, at the original cut of ANY of its records, the named effect was eligible for compensation; the transition reads only the named effect. The undo survives the base returning to 100 / null (R1 and R2; FA03 through close/reopen).
- B19 (D-B5-1; R8 :151, :156). Order-free coalescing: each group's records are sorted by op id, the least is the representative, every record is proof for the capture rule, the group sits at the earliest cut. Rows: two devices, other-device seq 1 and 100, and the other device's record holding the least op id.
- B20 (:103, :120, :154, N19c). Re-validation reads the lift under the display name as issued (reconstructed from the reason, lineage is identity). An earn and an adoption survive a rename under R1 and R2 and on the page.
- B21 (:148, :165, N07). The native host judges against the page's current basis (engineState may be a function; FA02 passes the adopted basis). After adoptBasis at 42.5 the held 45 offer answers STALE_OFFER, zero responses.
- B22 (:127, :148, :185). A v1 (host-shaped) completion whose Start captured a plan that differs from the current plan is refused PLAN_CHANGED at check, refs [its Close]; offers only (no fold write).
- PROPERTY (FC12 R7-PROPERTY, mulberry32, NATIVE_LOAD_PROPERTY_RUNS/SEED/REPORT/ALL). One lift, baseline or 100 start; actions: tops/misses (v2 and v1 with capture), checks, yeses, undos, re-checks, base moves including a return to the original, renames (one with ": "), plan edits, captures by a later Start, reopens, duplicate delivery from a second device, a second-device undo body from an older view. Invariants after every step: I1 no w without a yes, I2 a proven cancellation is kept and never re-offered, I3 equal under R1/R2 and both delivery orders (second device, causal chain, reversed counters), I4 rename-free, I5 a stale offer is never fresh.
  - Walk 1 (20,000 from 20260923): seeds 20269845, 20274085 (I3): an adoption issued on a HELD adoption's weight; red R7-P1 (r7\red-p1.txt A0153D19), fix: the dependent is held with it (R8 :156). Earlier: seed 20260961 (a ": " name; issued-name candidates). Walk 2 (20,000, dup/undo2 added): 0 counterexamples (r7\prop-20k-b.json 6E1B2938).
- Mutants (r7\mutants-summary.txt 9569CDB4, rerun r7\mutants-live-rerun.txt BFCF8C7B): 44 run, 41 KILLED (all r6 mutants re-anchored, plus R7 comp-reprice, issued-names, b22-filter, P1-dependent (also by the property row at 3000 runs), undo-all-records (by the least-op-id B19 rows), host-base-live, host-base-entry, and the FA03 variants). The representative cannot change an outcome because every record is proof, any record's eligibility validates, the cut is the minimum, and the transition reads only the named effect; a same-effect body at a later cut implies no same-lift training in between (else COMPENSATION_DESCENDANTS). The sort remains for determinism of ids.

## Round 8 (UNCOMMITTED on 4b83f4c; Claude l6 ACCEPT WITH NAMED DEBTS D-B6-1, D-B6-2)
Green: FC12 85 pass + 2 TODO (R8-P4, the STOP witness), FA03 24/24; with native-trend-context 150 tests, 147 pass, 2 TODO, 1 = the EXPOSED pin (r8\final-green.txt 501E4711). Every earlier row (B1-B22, R7-P1, R7-PROPERTY) is in these suites and green.
- D-B6-1 model (FC12 R8-PROPERTY, NATIVE_LOAD_PROPERTY8_RUNS/SEED/REPORT/ALL): adds fact corrections after the yes (mostly of consumed completions), technique forks, a second lift (alone or shared sessions), a legacy PROPOSED structural entry, wSets vector plans, the Close of a Start that captured the card earlier, host v1 sessions, and three unprovable delivery layouts (second device, no causal parents). I1 is an oracle over a ledger of every yes: no authority -> w/wSets exactly the plan's; 'adopted' -> a live adoption yes, w its target; 'landed' -> a live earn yes, landing reported, and a CLOSED session whose Start (or typed prescription) was the target after the yes; 'compensated' -> w is the prior image (live root yes's target, else the plan's), never the cancelled target. I6: the unprovable layouts (and R2) fold alike. I2-I5 as round 7, per lift.
- Walk (3 x 17,000 = 51,000 sequences, seeds 20261001, 1000001, 5000001; r8\p8-17k-*.json 3B458951, 80E59FDD, 426AD0EB): final run 0 counterexamples. Found and fixed on the way, red first: seeds 20261016, 20261036 (I3: a later legacy entry made R1 say RECORD_INVALID where R2's transition says LEGACY_PENDING; row R8-P2, r8\red-p2.txt; fix: a refusal the transition itself makes is reported by that name under every revision); seed 20261004 (I2: that held-back yes vanished from the spend index and its proven cancellation with it; row R8-P3, r8\red-p3.txt; fix: LEGACY_PENDING / VECTOR_ADOPTION_UNDEFINED at accept keep the spend, held unapplied, so no landing uses it and its cancellation still cancels it, R8 :121, :153, :156 "Keep accepted history"). Model-only corrections (no product change): the landing reads a typed prescription when the Start has no capture cell (seed 20261152); a corrected basis RETAINS its effect (:157); the compensated prior image is the root yes's target (seeds 20264309, 20265001, 20271302, 20270192).
- STOP (beyond R8, not fixed): seed 20273709 (and 5014268): a fork (any kind) or fact correction on Fx PRESS dated a shared session changes the engine's pooled noise comparator (progression.cjs typicalError pools every lift by its own era), so an Fx ROW yes no longer reproduces: R1 folds RECORD_INVALID, R2 applies it as written (:154). R8 makes the same-lift unordered base change one outcome under every revision (D7b) but is silent on another lift's unordered edit changing this lift's judgement through a pooled reader. Red on 4b83f4c product bytes with this round's tests: FC12 R8-P2/P3/P4 x2, R9-B23, R9-B24, R9-B24c, R9-B25 and FA03 R9-B23/B24/B25 (Astra's three callbacks, verbatim) fail (r9\red-4b83f4c-final.txt 2CB0503C). - Ruling (DECISIONS:793) as built in FC03, no format change: a recorded accept is judged ONLY at its original cut. Its issued basis already binds what the evaluator read (plan.programme_sha256 = every exercise record as projected, governor hold included; structural_queue_sha256; coverage of every fact op). The fold re-evaluates a record only when today's reconstruction of that cut matches those digests and covers exactly its fact ops; otherwise the inputs are not reproducible and a later edit never revokes consent: the body applies as written, as under an absent revision (R1 = R2). Newer plan authority stays with :156 (movedBase, dependents). Name guessing (issuedNames) and issued-equipment restoring are removed. PRODUCER_REVISION and the issuance shape are UNCHANGED (native-load.cjs untouched; no new issuance field).

R9-FORGED: a re-digested tampered adoption under a governor hold still folds RECORD_INVALID (re-validation runs whenever the cut is reproduced).
- B25: the Start capture's reps cell is the per-set rep TARGET (engine-capture.cjs), not the window; a captured target above the current window top proves the window moved down since completion and a new check refuses PLAN_CHANGED [Close Ref], typed and host slots alike, taking precedence over FC01 readers after its step 2 (R9-B25, FA03 Astra callback). LIMIT (for the PM): a window change that keeps every captured target inside the new window (e.g. raised 10 -> 12) is not provable from the capture; FC01 then answers WINDOW_NOT_TOP, never an offer. Full binding needs the window itself in the capture (engine-capture.cjs, outside this grant).

- B28 RESTORE lost on replay (FC01 compensate):
  - A compensation is classified by its OWN shape. RESTORE means its target differs from its own base.
  - A RESTORE of an adoption that is unapplied on this replay (held, e.g. a later admitted base with no ordering op) writes the adoption's recorded base_load.fields (w, wSets). Both responses and the tombstone are kept.
  - A RETIRE of an unapplied effect writes nothing.
  - Unchanged: an applied adoption is still reverted from its authority's prior image, whatever the record's shape. This is how R7-B18 (an undo survives a return to the original base) stays green. Interpretation, flagged.

  - Rows: FC12 R16-B29 (C3 PROVISIONAL, then C4 ORD consuming exactly [C3, C4]) and FA03 R16-B29 (the same on the real host).
  - Walk invariant I13. No earn offer consumes a sighting that a recorded yes spent, whether live or undone. An earn with a single root is either the EARLY proposal ("OFF ONE SIGHTING") or carries a noise margin over the lift's prior line.
  - The pilot showed that "one root means one-sighting copy" is false, because the noise-margin DEBUT reuses the ordinary copy (seeds 1000308, 1001060). The rule was corrected to the margin form.

## Round 16b (UNCOMMITTED, same worktree): R7-comp-reprice, R7-comp-any and R7-canon-cut closed
- Premise P, restated: at a reproducible cut, the cut digests cover every input of an undo body. It holds, and here is the proof, read from FC01 compensation() :336-369 and evaluate() :168-187:
  - The body's basis is json(req.basis), and at replay req.basis is the record's own basis. So effect_frontier is an ECHO, not an input to recompute. lift, compensates and spend_id come from the request too.
  - base_load and target_load are read from the lift's exercise (FIELDS, sets, native_load_authority) and from the queue.
  - Replay evaluates withFacts(V, cut) before the governor. sameCut digests the exercises after the governor and the queue as they are. The governor writes only holdFlag (:400-402), which is not in FIELDS. So digest equality implies every read field is equal.
  - The rows (the lift's completions at the cut) decide only offer versus refusal, never the body.
  - Hence a GENUINE record at a reproducible cut re-evaluates to exactly its own body.
- P was true, but the product was defective. The loose comparison (same compensates) also let a FORGED body pass re-validation: a genuine RETIRE, re-shaped into a RESTORE to the prior 100 and re-digested, applied under the present revision. And `records.some` let one genuine record vouch for a forged copy of the same cancellation.
- Red first, FC12 r16\red16b-fc12.txt: R16b-COMP-REPRICE and R16b-COMP-EVERY fail on the round-16 code. Both forgeries applied silently.
- Fix, FC03 fold re-validation of compensations: EVERY record must re-evaluate to EXACTLY its own body (`records.every`, `same(o.body, b)`). Genuine records are unaffected, by P. Green: FC12 135/135, FA03 41/41.
- Under R2 (revision absent), a forged undo body still applies as written after S1-S8 and DERIVABLE. That is the spec's absent-revision rule (:155), and DERIVABLE bounds such a body to RETIRE or to the adoption's prior image. R16b-COMP-REPRICE pins this R2 outcome so a reviewer can see it.
- R7-canon-cut: R16b-CANON-CUT, green on head; its red is the mutant.
  - Input: a re-digested copy of a genuine undo that claims the empty cut.
  - Product: the cancellation sits at the earliest claimed cut (review B19), before the adoption it names, so it is refused RECORD_INVALID naming both records. The adoption stands. R1 and R2.
  - Mutant: it uses the representative's cut, applies the genuine undo and never examines the forged copy.
  - Why genuine records cannot tell the two placements apart, which is why only a forgery kills it:
    - FC01 refuses an undo when any later completion of the lift is in its cut (:350), so no same-lift completion or descendant accept can lie between two genuine records' cuts.
    - A Start that could land the target lies at or after every record's cut.
    - Other lifts' events are independent.
  - Disclosed consequence: a forged early-cut copy spoils that cancellation, and its later undo records join the spoiled group. The lift is held RECORD_INVALID, and exit (b), adopt-baseline after training, still resolves it (NO TRAP).
- Mutants: 177, meaning the round-16 set, with R7-comp-reprice re-anchored to the new strict line (its mutation is now "strict to loose"), plus R16b-comp-some and R16b-comp-some-host ("every to some").
- Results on the final bytes:
  - Walk: 3 x 17,000 = 51,000 walks, 0 counterexamples. The report bytes are identical to round 16 (42866456, 94D79A9C, E1CB1FBF). So no genuine walk record behaves differently under the strict every-record re-validation, which is empirical support for P.
  - Mutants: r16\mutants-summary.txt 9F5D26BF, 177 in total, 174 killed by behaviour. R7-canon-cut, R7-comp-any, R7-comp-reprice and R16b-comp-some are killed in FC12. R16-registrar-source and the others are unchanged. The round-16 summary is kept as mutants-summary-r16.txt.
  - The 3 LIVE mutants are all host-only reachability, and the FC12 variant of each is killed:
    - R11-window-legacy-host: as before.
    - R7-comp-reprice-host and R16b-comp-some-host: the guarded host never carries a record whose body is not a genuine issuance. A rewritten record is refused LOCAL_HISTORY_IDENTITY_UNPROVEN (FA03 D-FRESH-2), and the host records only a body equal to a fresh offer (sameIssued). By P, a genuine record re-evaluates to exactly its own body, so on the host the strict and loose comparisons, and "every" and "some", cannot differ.
  - R12-decode-absent is still killed only by the byte pin.
  - Red: r16\red16b-fc12.txt 00F0E080. Green: FC12 135/135 (g7-fc12.txt C1A2F6AB), FA03 41/41 (g5-fa03.txt E7335AEB).
  - Full local set (80 files): 0 new failures vs ab445c6 (r16\full-compare-16b.txt 8C94BD33).
- Round-16b sha256 (only FC03 and FC12 changed since round 16):
  - native-load-effects.cjs e985908a48e9030ab266cc8e3d90ddcc28e27838c62f6fd40ba6e0d3fc6ffbf5 (971);
  - FC12 test 18687ed232a37a1690dd82ed387d325469ca049b0bcc921cb6b257079146034f (2953);
  - the others as in round 16. All LF, no added non-ASCII.

## Round 17 (UNCOMMITTED on e04b8e6, which holds rounds 16/16b; spec R9.7 912c36c (file sha256 da1398e1; "811a322d" was wrong, see 17a); Fable l8 ACCEPT WITH NAMED DEBTS)
- Red first. FC12 r17\red-fc12.txt: R17-ANCHOR-STRUCTURAL and R17-DEAD-YES fail on e04b8e6.

- D-L8F-4, structural miss verification (FC03 correspondence, spec R9.7 :155). For adopt-baseline with a numeric capture, each authority_ref must be one of:
  - the consumed Close, where the consumed Start captured this lift's selected native entry and the S4-bound actual loads differ from that entry's target; or
  - a proposal-response of this lift named by an active hold of the lift at this point in the fold.
  - Anything else refuses RECORD_INVALID base_load. The fold hands correspondence its issues, spent and groups.
  - The selected native entry is read from the fold's ACCEPTED records: an uncancelled, unlanded earn of this lift whose target vector is the capture. The first version read the replay-time queue instead. Walk attempt 1 found seed 20268799 against it: a legacy PROPOSED entry admitted later holds the Q yes back (LEGACY_PENDING), so no queue entry remained. - Interpretations a reviewer should check against the spec:
  1. Exit (b) is adopt-baseline only. An earlier adopt-observed on a held weight stays its dependent (R7-P1, spec :161).
  2. "After" is proven by causality or by one device's own sequence (:161). A fold-side exit also accepts a holding record proven before the adopted completion's Start, when that Start precedes the response (R13-EXIT-ORDER).
  3. An exit records the projection (w null) as its base. A later base-w change neither conflicts with it nor stales an offer rooted in it, and its Undo restores w null (:161).
  4. RECORD_INVALID holds apply from the invalid record's place in the log. A record proven before every copy of it is not held back.
  5. :162 applies to completions not proven after the holds. A completion after the holds that captured a numeric card, which the host cannot produce, keeps the hold refusal.
  6. Legacy entries: hidden only in the registered projection. The check and the fold still see them, so LEGACY_PENDING is named.
  7. B28: a RESTORE of an UNAPPLIED adoption writes the adoption's recorded base_load.fields; a RESTORE or RETIRE of an APPLIED adoption still reverts it from its authority's prior image (R7-B18 stays green). So a RESTORE's weight persists over any later admitted base that has no ordering op, and it raises no issue.
  8. MISSED DEBUT: FC03, not FC01, fills load_basis.authority_refs when it issues an exit (the spec names FC01). FC01 reads them only for the step-2 exception.
  9. D-FRESH-2: the guarded host cannot hold a forged-source record, so the host row asserts that refusal plus a static pin; the fold difference itself is shown in FC12.
- Model conventions in the walk, each commented in the file:
  - a baseline-ask Start names the plan ops it folded as causal parents;
  - a second-device copy names the ops it synced;
  - the two-device layout's device-B sequence follows its causal chain;
  - exit ledger entries follow interpretation 3;
  - I10 skips only an unattributable hold and a completion dated before a declared fork;
  - an exit response names the plan ops its device folded (round 16);
  - a w-only edit under a RESTORE stales nothing (I5, round 16);
  - I13 accepts a one-root earn only as the EARLY proposal or with a positive rep margin over the prior line (a necessary condition of earn.cjs:75-76's noise clear, not the full rule).
- LIVE mutants after round 16b: 3 of 177, all host-only reachability (see round 16b). Superseded round-16 list, kept for the record: (5 of 175), with the argument a reviewer can check, or where there is none. R7-canon-order and R11-S8-adopt, listed as equivalent before, were NOT equivalent; both are killed now (see round 16).
  - R11-window-legacy-host (host variant only; the FC12 and PROPERTY variants are killed). Argument: this is reachability, not equivalence. engine-capture.cjs:91 writes window_hi whenever the plan's hi is a positive safe integer, and every FA03 installation Start is captured through it with hi set, so no host input reaches the legacy-cell branch.
  - R7-comp-reprice and -host (the re-evaluated undo must equal the record's body exactly, instead of naming the same compensates). Argument, conditional on premise P: this path runs only when the cut is reproducible, meaning the same producer revision and identical programme digest, structural-queue digest and fact coverage. FC01 is a pure function of those inputs plus the request, which the record carries. So the re-evaluated offer equals the recorded body, and both comparisons agree. P is that every input to an undo body is covered by those digests. I have not proven P for basis.effect_frontier.
  - R7-comp-any (reproducibility judged on the first undo record only). Under the same premise P: when the first record is reproducible it re-evaluates to an offer naming its compensates, and the fold proceeds exactly as on the apply-as-written path. Only the representative body is transitioned in either case, and the capture guard reads every record in both. Not proven beyond P.
  - R7-canon-cut (an undo group placed at its representative's cut instead of the earliest record's). No equivalence argument. A difference needs an accept or Close event between the two cuts that interacts with the cancelled effect, for example a descendant yes recorded concurrently on a second device. No such row has been built. Treat it as an uncovered gap, not as equivalent.
  - R12-decode-absent: killed only by the byte pin. It is equivalent under R9.2+, because c1 compares the wrappers, so ABSENT and present-null never reach decode unequal.
- Residuals and named debts (spec :392): D-R9-RECOVERY-SCOPE, D-R9-ADMISSION (the actual import path, FC09/FC10 unbuilt), D-R9-CAPTURE, D-R9-EDIT/RECOVERY, D-R9-DELIVERY.
  - Host-unreachable cases are shown in FC12 only: the early-cut record (T2 refuses it), a second device, and a numeric card closed after a hold.
  - A lift the athlete never trains again stays held; this is harmless (:163).

  - r16\mutants.ps1: the mutants (r16\anchors.cjs checks that every anchor is unique);

- Round 17c map (Astra L10 at c0695e0, spec R9.8 105cc28; details in the Round 17c section). Only FC12 changed; the product, FA03 and PRODUCER_REVISION are unchanged.

Step 3: adoption unchanged; before earn(), a MISSED CLOSE refuses PLAN_CHANGED [Close Ref]. adoptReason names the captured debut card and the kept working weight (PROPOSED copy, below). landing(): SELECTED ENTRY (a typed prescribed_load must agree) else DEBUT_BASIS_UNPROVEN completion; exact = every original slot performed at its target (scalar: newW at any count; vector: its accepted layout only); otherwise the entry is consumed: done, 'MISSED', native_load_missed_by, effect 'missed' with close_ref, nothing else written.

### Step 5: R8 walk oracle (K R8 WALK ORACLE)
- held() drops isMissedHold. Debut cards are trained at the target (landing), 5 lb light (the working weight: PLAN_CHANGED), 2.5 lb light or 5 lb heavy (equal other load: adopt-observed offered), or with the last set 5 lb light (mixed: VECTOR_ADOPTION_UNDEFINED; on a vector plan every non-plan load is). Set-count class: a scalar debut's count is edited to 2 or 4 between the yes and the Start (inside 'train', and a 'sets' action), cards and captures follow the count; the I1 landed check uses SELECTED ENTRY.
- I14 rewritten: a Close of the pending debut's card consumes it (ESTABLISH iff exact, else MISSED by that Close); a miss holds nothing, writes none of w, wSets, wAt, last, never re-prescribes the target; the check offers exactly [adopt-observed of the actual loads] claiming exactly [that Close's Ref], or PLAN_CHANGED / VECTOR_ADOPTION_UNDEFINED [Close Ref]; its yes sets w and leaves the entry MISSED; its Undo is covered by I1 (compensated branch). PREFIX_UNRESOLVED is not generated by this walk (no skipped slot class).
- Corrections class: a missed debut Close's loads are sometimes corrected to its card (it then lands); every claimed yes and every A1 yes is never RECORD_INVALID at any step (l11 A1, A2). A1 class: after a miss, a later training lifts the old target on the working card (adopted), then another equal load on that target's card; the check and yes follow.
- Trace names: missed-consumed, missed-observed-offered, missed-observed-yes, missed-plan-changed, missed-unequal, debut-landed, setcount-landed, setcount-missed, sets-before-debut, correct-load, missed-corrected-landed, a1-setup-yes, a1-unclaimed-yes.

### Step 6: findings from the first 17000-walk run (named for the PM; product unchanged by them)
- The first R8 run (seed 20261001, 17000 walks, oracle before this step) found 3 counterexamples (r18\p18-17k-20261001.json 944BD959). Walks were stopped there (seed 1000001 killed, own lock removed), the oracle was narrowed as below, and every walk was rerun.
- D-R9.9-LATER-HOLD-UNDO (seeds 20264386, 20266433; I2): a claimed adoption and its Undo are recorded; a later correction of a Q-consumed completion makes Q BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED, so the missed Close no longer consumes Q; the claimed adoption meets TARGET_QUEUED at its point ("refused, not applied, and not in the spend index", J LATER HOLD), so its recorded Undo names no fold spend and folds RECORD_INVALID compensates, a hold of the lift. No weight moves (w stays the plan's), exit (b) still resolves it. This is J's stated consequence carried one step further by the unchanged DERIVABLE rule; R9.9 does not say what an already recorded Undo becomes. Scratch probe (FC12 compiled in memory with two probe rows; output r18\probes.txt 2D714650): issues [BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED [fx-resp-1], TARGET_QUEUED [fx-resp-3], RECORD_INVALID compensates [fx-resp-4]], w 100, Q pending, the adoption not in the spend index; R1 and R2. The walk's I2 exempts exactly this case (target absent from the spend index AND a TARGET_QUEUED issue naming its yes); trace 'later-hold-undo'. PM question: should such a refused claimed yes keep its spend (as a held-back record) so that its recorded Undo cancels it?
- D-R18-SETS-RESTORE (seed 20266884; I2; pre-existing, NOT missed-debut semantics, outside the grant): an APPLIED adoption, then a set-count edit (sets 3 -> 2), then its Undo: FC01 compensation issues a RESTORE whose target vector is projected at the NEW count ([100,100]) while the adoption's recorded base_load vector has the old count ([100,100,100]); the fold's DERIVABLE RESTORE check then refuses the athlete's consented Undo RECORD_INVALID target_load, the adopted 105 stays applied and the lift is held. Probe: w 105, authority adopted, RECORD_INVALID target_load [fx-resp-2], R1 and R2. Reachable on the host through a sets-only plan edit (plan-edit-commands.cjs:46). Fixing it changes FC01 compensation or DERIVABLE outside DECISIONS:796 (c): not changed here, STOP item for the PM. The walk's set-count class now edits the count once, only between a scalar debut's yes and its Start (K's class), never back, so it no longer straddles an adoption and its Undo.
- Walk oracle defect, pre-existing, exposed by the new RNG paths (seed 5009774; I5): the window/cache branch compared a HELD lift's last offer (whose base is the held projection, effShown, since round 13) with the unprojected plan (effOf), so every exit looked stale after a window or cache edit that changed nothing. Red: r18\red-r8-5009774.txt 6E0BB5CC (found 1, "I5 stale offer still fresh after a window or cache edit"). Fix (test only): compare with effShown when the offer was held, as the plan-edit branch does. Green: r18\green-r8-5009774.txt 078C5DF0 (found 0). This fix only narrows when that branch checks (the new condition implies the old one; the check writes nothing and draws no random number), so the 17000-walk runs of seeds 20261001 and 1000001 made on the bytes before it (0 counterexamples) stand; seed 5000001 was rerun on the final bytes.

### 1. STOP-R20B1-1/-2 walk ruling (b)
- Generator (FC12 propertySequence8, action 'legacy'): a draw on a lift whose m.base w is null is PROPOSED only (the state draw is kept, then overridden, so every seed keeps its action sequence); a draw on a lift with B.wSets carries newWSets = wSets.map(x=>x+(newW-w)). The comment states both invariants with their citations (E/writers.cjs:383, E/progression.cjs:365, FC01:501/:549; E/earn.cjs:63/:88, FC01:516-517) and names D-R20-RESTORE-OVER-LEGACY as not excluded.
- R20-LEGACY-NEEDS-LOAD (green-kept): the three earnWalk arms that queue at w 100 (DEBUT :88, PROPOSED :97, PROPOSED :63) queue nothing at w null; nextLoad null at w null; the N02c earn body re-recorded over a null w is refused RECORD_INVALID base_load (FC01:501) where the body as issued queues (:549); the w-null lift is offered no earn. Killed by c-fc01-501 (refused at target_load instead; m-501 4bfb6aef) and c-prog-365 (the :97 arm queues at w null; m-365 45698379).
- R20-LEGACY-VECTOR-SHIFT (green-kept): on wSets [100,100,95] each arm queues newWSets [105,105,100]; N11's DEBUT 105 [105,100] applies and queues its vector; the same body with newWSets null is refused RECORD_INVALID candidate (FC01:516-517). Killed by c-earn-88-noshift (m-88 bea80a7a) and c-fc01-517 (applied instead; m-517 eabdc07c).
- R8 shard, 17000 walks from 20261001, 4 shared shards of 4250 (20261001, 20265251, 20269501, 20273751; fin-r8-* e1977781, 63847eef, ae76e8ba, a26fa397; byte-equal to a pre-row run of the same walk code, w1-r8-*). No new class: no I1, I2, I3, I14, I16, I17, CARD-PARITY; the 11 I6 are STOP-R20B2-3's seeds (20262146, 20262505, 20262924, 20264651, 20264831, 20268120, ...).

### 2. D-R20-RESTORE-OVER-LEGACY (carried debt row)
- FC12 has no known-red convention for carried debts, so R20-RESTORE-OVER-LEGACY asserts the CURRENT refused output and names the debt and R9.13 in its title (it turns red when the fix lands). Fixture: base w null, C1 at 60, [adopt-baseline 60] yes (applied), its RESTORE Undo (base 60, target null) recorded while applied, then the base re-admitted at w 100 with a pending legacy DEBUT 105. Measured, typed v2 and host v1, R1 and R2: w null with the legacy DEBUT 105 pending and the capture refuses ENGINE_CAPTURE_BASELINE_UNPROVEN through cardLoads. Controls: not re-admitted, the baseline ask captures; re-admitted without the Undo, the moved base holds the adoption (EFFECT_CONFLICT load_basis [rl-y1], w 100) and the held projection hides the legacy entry, so the baseline ask captures. The advisor's path is real as stated.

### 3. D-R21L1-2: spec R9.13 (viii) WALK-I3-PARTIAL-ORDER
- (a) Precondition. FC12 reads FC03's own provenBefore (and its const text) out of the product file and evaluates them as written (the ALV precedent); provenPairs lists every ordered pair (a, b) with provenBefore([a], b); orderKept compares the list before and after a transformed log and throws TRANSFORM_ORDER_CHANGED (not a counterexample, so the walk aborts loudly, never a skip).
- FINDING (measured, test model only): the round-21a R8 transform FAILS the precondition at the first default seed 20261001: 26 pairs added, 0 dropped (fx-close-1 .. fx-start-4 < fx-p-3; fx-p-3 is a device-B dup whose causal parents name fx-p-1) (out\r8-default.txt). Cause: provenBefore (FC03:287) is not transitive across a device's own sequence. It walks only causal_parents and device_predecessor_op_id, and it compares device_seq only between the two end points. Adding each op's same-device predecessor as a causal parent therefore makes every earlier op of that device proven before any op on another device that names one of its ops as a parent. The 21a closure argument (and Fable's check of it) used transitive happens-before, not the rule's function. So the brief's prescribed form ("keep each op's causal parents plus its same-original-device predecessor") preserves provenBefore only when no op names a cross-device causal parent (true in R7, false in R8).
- Fix (test bytes; the rule's own permission "move an op to another device only where that adds no pair"): one shared deliveryTransform for R7 and R8. Each plan op of an original device d may move, in original order, to device 'fx-2dev|'+d (sequence 1000+i). A moved op gains as causal parents the ops of d below it that stay; each op of d above it that stays gains it. All plan ops move when provenBefore stays identical; otherwise ops are admitted one at a time in (device, sequence) order, each kept only if the whole relation stays identical; orderKept is then asserted. The walks tally twoDevice:moved and twoDevice:kept. No invariant, seed, assertion or generator changed.
- R8 red -> green: seed 20261001 red (21a transform + precondition) -> green (moved 4, kept 2); Astra's seed 13030374 green (moved 4, kept 14); default 100 seeds 0 counterexamples (moved 188, kept 70).
- (b) R7 adds order: the round-18 form (one global device_seq chain, every plan op put on 'fx-device-B', the device the dup and undo2 ops already use) FAILS the precondition at seed 20260960: 64 pairs added, 0 dropped (the dups fx-p-2 and fx-p-3 became ordered against every main-device op) (out\r7-default-oldtransform.txt). Fixed by the same deliveryTransform: seed 20260960 green (moved 8, kept 0); default 150 seeds 0. R7 logs carry no causal parents, so every plan op moves (kept 0 in all 17000 bound seeds).
- (c) R7 bound on the current FC03: 17000 seeds from 20260923, ALL=1, 4 shared shards of 4250: 0 counterexamples of any class (I1 0, I2 0, I3 0, I4 0, I5 0) (w7-20260923 1be47c64, w7-20265173 79dee628, w7-20269423 3303582a, w7-20273673 c43c76bb; moved 9380, kept 0; dup 9871, undo2-yes 89, undo-restore 382). Control: the same 17000 under the round-18 transform restored in memory (overlay, no precondition): also 0 (o7-* 9bde03d7, d3351334, 173cc3c1, 36d19f15). Nothing was dropped or exempted.
- Spec (viii)'s re-run of Astra's interval on the new R8 transform: 13024001..13041000 (17000, 4 shards of 4250, ALL=1): 0 counterexamples of any class (w8-13024001 af0c2f13, w8-13028251 cac971c7, w8-13032501 92add271, w8-13036751 0abb3010), seed 13030374 inside it. Plan ops moved 31650, kept 7170 (18%): a kept op is compared in its original layout, so I3 has less reach on those ops than a full move would give; this is the most the rule permits under provenBefore.
- The three R8 bounds re-run on the new transform (not in the brief; the walk evidence of record for (i), (iv) and (v) was measured on the 21a transform): 51000 seeds, 12 shared shards of 4250, ALL=1: 0 counterexamples of any class. 20261001 (0f7609f3, b520a338, b2f10fe0, 54aed046), 1000001 (d8813d52, 7fc02fcc, 561336ee, 5a90a5d9), 5000001 (04ecaf6e, 34a417cd, fc8bb5b6, 27c5e15c); plan ops moved 94168, kept 22436.

### Pointers
- NAMED LIMIT, not a new debt (Fable R22 l5 Q2 and Q7, recommended to be recorded under this pointer): the multi-entry capture refusal. W/engine-capture.cjs :72-73 in the fa68a748 bytes (`const selected=card.isDebutNow?input.queue.filter(q=>q.exId===card.id&&!q.done&&['debut','unlock'].includes(q.kind)):[]; if(selected.length>1)fail('ENGINE_CAPTURE_LOAD_MAPPING_REQUIRED');`, the comment "Multiple matching moves do not prove which load vector won" at :70-71) refuses the whole day when a debut-now lift carries more than one unfinished debut/unlock entry, with or without R9.13 (v)'s hide. Spec 7ef8291 :81 names it as a DEPENDENCY: "W/engine-capture.cjs:69-70 refuses ENGINE_CAPTURE_LOAD_MAPPING_REQUIRED when the day's card is a debut (card.isDebutNow) and the lift has more than one unfinished debut/unlock entry, PROPOSED included. Its repair is the THIRD engine change, owner-held (DECISIONS:639, :644 D2; a7b0e24 Revision 3). This spec does not order, design or assume it." (the spec's :69-70 is :72-73 in these bytes; the R9.10 FIT hunk moved it). Its repair is outside grants (a)-(f); R22L4-KEPT-ORDER-OVER-NULL part (a) and L15-B4-EVERY-ENTRY-ALV already carry it as measured (twin equality: the hide neither causes nor cures it). Round 22e's citation ":76-77" (row comment and report) and round 23's "NEW item ... stays the PM's call" are superseded by this pointer; the DECISIONS line numbers are the spec's, not re-read from this seat.

### 1. Rows (FC12 265 -> 267; FA03 56 unchanged; each cites its clause in its title and asserts the specified outcome; every value invented)
- R22L5-EXERCISE-OWN-STD-CONJUNCT (FC12 266; D-R22L5-1, mutant k08 "(ex.std && ex.own) -> ex.own" at FC01 :223; spec B's ACTIVE own branch, E/writers.cjs:279 the same conjunction; R9.13 (v) "FC01's LEGACY_PENDING rules ... are unchanged"): fx-press at w 100 with no queue entry and own true WITHOUT std (and std [100,100,95] WITHOUT own), one normal completion on the 100 card, typed v2 and host v1 x R1 and R2 -> THE CHECK is not LEGACY_PENDING; it equals the check over the same completion with no branch at all (status, code, refs, field); and it is exactly refused NATIVE_LOAD_PROVISIONAL [its Close Ref] field null (the ordinary first top, N03a), as Fable's probe measured (fc12-p00 head / fc12-p08 k08, v2 R1). The v1 form builds the Start's immutable capture with the 100 card's load and reps cells through the existing r22eCaptureBy helper (fc16Capture's shape).
- R22L5-DONE-LEGACY-OVER-NULL-SHOWN (FC12 267; Fable l5 INFO, mutant w16 "!q.done && " dropped from FC03 :271 hide; R9.13 (v) RULE "unfinished" / "not done", "Only the registered projection changes"): a never-held fx-press at w null with a DONE legacy DEBUT 60 (done true, state ESTABLISH) and, the same, a DONE legacy UNLOCK 65, R1 and R2 -> the fold keeps it as admitted, no issue; the registered projection keeps it ([kind, true, ESTABLISH, newW]) and its queue deep-equals the fold queue (nothing hidden); the fold state is unchanged by the projection; the card is the baseline ask [null,null,null] and the day prepares.
- INFO row decision: ADDED, because the outcome is unambiguous from the spec: (v) hides only entries that are "unfinished"/"not done", "Only the registered projection changes", so a done:true legacy debut/unlock of a never-held w-null lift stays in the registered projection; with no unfinished entry the card is the existing baseline ask (E/today.cjs:103, the same card L15-B6 pins). Measured on these bytes, round 23's L15-B6-FINISHED-ENTRY-SHOWN (FC12 265, done 1 / 'yes') and FA03 L15-B6-FINISHED-ENTRY-HOST (56) also kill w16 (Fable's l5 ran on the round-22e bytes, before round 23); this row adds the plain boolean done:true and the unlock kind at the unheld w-null quantifier.
- Helpers: none added at top level (brief, at and mine are row-local); the rows reuse effectsGate, C, TOP, e, v1Of, F0, foldArgs, checkOf, ref, r22eCaptureBy, exOf, LEGQ, b2Issues, b39Card, R1 and R2.
- Reachability (static, unchanged standing): own without std and std without own are not produced by any rebuild writer (every rebuild-side clearing site clears both together, E/writers.cjs :263, :272, :286, measured by Select-String on that file); a done legacy queue entry is a finished-history shape the old app's writers produce (E/writers.cjs:288 sets done true and state OWNED on the own entry it closes; a done debut/unlock is the same shape, not traced to a writer from this seat). The old app's src is not readable from this seat.

- EQUIVALENT (33), each with its proof (class.txt):
  - A02: the product call site: replay() passes prep.candidateState() (m4/import/replay-core.cjs:52-53 parseStrictJson(JSON.stringify(migrated)), :87 a structuredClone); a null state makes replay throw a TypeError at source-admission.mjs:495 (`state.reads`) under the head exactly as the mutant throws at :851, so replay's outcome is the same; only FC12's direct call differs (head [], mutant TypeError). A04: the same (the mutant throws at :851 reading state.queue).
  - A05: each conversion reads only q and its lift and writes only q.newWSets, which no other entry's test reads (the skip reads q's own newWSets), so the converted state is identical in either order; only the ORDER of the returned named list differs, and the sole product call site (:493 `legacyVectorAdmission(state);`) discards it. A52, A54: change only the `w` field (undefined for null when ex.w is ABSENT) or the aliasing of the `wSets` field of a returned named record; :493 discards the return value.
  - A08: the admitted state is JSON (replay-core.cjs:52-53); a truthy non-object JSON queue member (number, string, true) has no own `kind`, so (q.kind!=='debut'&&q.kind!=='unlock') holds and the mutant continues too; only a function-valued member (not JSON) would differ.
  - A21, A36, A42: Number.isFinite(v) is false for every v whose typeof is not 'number' (ECMA-262 Number.isFinite step 1), so the dropped typeof test is implied by the isFinite test beside it.
  - A23, A38, A43, A50: each differs only for a NaN or +-Infinity newW, w or wSets element (A50: `typeof w!=='number'&&!Number.isFinite(w)` equals the head's pair for every non-number and every finite number); the admitted state is JSON round-tripped (replay-core.cjs:52-53), and JSON has no non-finite number.
  - P25: fold.issues never holds a falsy element: every FC03 push is an object literal or an `issue` object (FC03 :583, :736, :782, :809, :817, :861, :868, :924, :936, :945) and refusedFold (:550) wraps one; both product callers of heldProjection pass a foldNativeLoad result (L/today-bindings.mjs:548, :677). P32, P34: heldProjection is never called with a null or undefined fold (the same two callers). P33: a fold that reaches heldProjection always carries its state: :548 runs only when fold.status is 'ready', and foldNativeLoad returns state null only with status 'refused' (FC03 :550, :952); :677 guards p.fold.state.
  - P42 (argument): dropping `typeof q.native_load_spend !== 'string'` from the :271 hide changes only an UNFINISHED NATIVE entry on an UNHELD lift whose fold w is null or ABSENT, a shape spec (v) states is excluded ("the R9.11 INVARIANT at :158 already excludes an unfinished native entry on a w-null lift"): native entries enter the fold only through an accept (:150), FC01 refuses an earn over a non-numeric w (:156 DERIVABLE (c3)), a later w-null base holds the lift by the moved-base test (:165), and an exit retires the lift's native entries while an Undo past a later earn refuses COMPENSATION_DESCENDANTS (:154). The builder did not write a synthetic row for an input the spec declares unreachable; the PM may rule otherwise.
  - P53: when no entry satisfies hide, filter keeps every entry in order, so {...state, queue: copy} deep-equals state; both product callers use the value only (:548 spreads it, :677 structuredClones it).
  - K12: refs are refsOf-normalised {op_id, commitment} (FC01 :85); `sorted` orders their JSON strings by code unit, which orders by op_id code units unless one op_id is a proper prefix of another followed by a code unit below U+0022 (space, '!' or a control character), and byOp (:27) orders by op_id code units and is stable, so it leaves that order unchanged (equal op_ids keep the commitment order in both).
  - K13: the check's effect_frontier is built by FC03 basisOf as (spent || []).map((x) => ({ spend_id, response_refs, close_ref })) (FC03 :326): every element is a map.
  - K14: only FC01 :648 writes an authority carrying close_op_id, always with kind 'landed' (:566 'adopted' carries none), and a compensation restores a prior image written by one of these two, so `auth.kind === 'landed'` is implied by `auth.close_op_id === cur.close`. K19: a truthy non-map native_load_authority (a JSON array, string, number or true) has no own `kind`, so nothing is refused either way.
  - K25: :212-:216 refuse TARGET_QUEUED for every q && q.exId === lift && !q.done with a string native_load_spend, so at :222 the dropped conjunct always holds. K46: :220 refuses unless idx === rows.length - 1, so at :226 rows.slice(0, idx + 2) equals rows.slice(0, idx + 1). K49: the forks come from E.resetForksOf (E/plan.cjs:31-33), which filters out every falsy member.
  - H03 (argument): a proposal-response op is written only as class 'plan' (spec :36, the guarded durable command); a tampered op fails the log's identity check, so project() is not ok and both return null (FA03 D-FRESH-2). H04 (argument): only a proposal-response op carries payload.proposal_id together with a payload.issuance JSON-equal to a host-held one ([NO-ONLY] automatic records carry no proposal_id). H07 (argument): proposal_id is proposalDigest(producer, body, reason) of the issuance (NativeLoadEffects.issuanceFor; FA03 D-FRESH-2 recomputes it that way), so a JSON-equal issuance implies an equal proposal_id; a stored proposal_id that disagrees with its issuance fails the log's identity check.
  - H09 (argument): on a log written by this installation (no sync, imports refused, :157) every non-rejected proposal-response was committed only after a fresh equal offer (:149; respond :714-716), and a committed accept keeps its spend in every later fold (applied, held back, conflicted or repair-marked: :158, :165, :166), so "found" implies "folded".
  - H16: the returned state is value-identical; project() refolds from the repository and the basis on every call (FC03 withFacts JSON-clones the base, :93), so the returned object is shared with neither the page's basis nor a later call. H30: when p.ok is false, project() returns {ok:false, code} with no fold, so `!p.fold` holds and savedResponse returns null either way. H31: savedResponse's two callers test truthiness only (:711 `if (already)`, :727 `if (late)`).

### 5. SPEC-SILENT (19; live on the final bytes; NO row written; each for a PM ruling, with the exact input and both outputs)
- S24-A01 (SA :851, `Array.isArray` dropped for exercises): input {exercises: {} (a non-array object), queue: [a pending legacy DEBUT 105 of fx-press]}. Head: exercises read as [], nothing converted or named, returns []. Mutant: TypeError "exercises.find is not a function". (iv) quantifies over S.exercises members; a non-array exercises is not a shape it names.
- S24-A03 (SA :851, `Array.isArray` dropped for queue): input {exercises: [fx-press], queue: {} (a non-array object)}. Head: returns []. Mutant: TypeError "queue is not iterable".
- S24-A07 (SA :853, `!q||` dropped): input queue [null, a pending legacy DEBUT 105 of fx-press w 100 wSets [100,100,95]]. Head: skips null and converts the entry to [105,105,100]. Mutant: TypeError reading 'done' of null (typeof null is 'object'). A null queue member is JSON and not a shape (iv) names.
- S24-A29 (SA :855, `x&&` dropped in find): input exercises [null, fx-press w 100 wSets [100,100,95]] and its pending legacy DEBUT 105. Head: [105,105,100]. Mutant: TypeError reading 'id' of null.
- S24-A30 (SA :855, find -> findLast): input two exercises with id fx-press (first w 100 wSets [100,100,95], then w 90 wSets [90,85,85]) and a pending legacy DEBUT 105. Head: converts from the first, [105,105,100]. Mutant: from the last, [105,100,100]. Duplicate lift ids are not a shape (iv) names (FC01 refuses LIFT_UNRESOLVED for them, E/native-load.cjs:198).
- S24-P05 (FC03 :255, `x &&` dropped in projectHeld's exercises map): input fold state exercises [null, fx-press] with an active hold on fx-press. Head: keeps null, projects fx-press w/wSets null. Mutant: TypeError reading 'id' of null.
- S24-P09 (FC03 :256, `q &&` dropped in projectHeld's queue filter): input fold state queue [null, ...] with an active hold on any lift. Head: keeps null. Mutant: TypeError reading 'exId' of null.
- S24-P13 (FC03 :256, projectHeld's legacy disjunct dropped) and S24-P35 (FC03 :269, the registered call with legacy false): input a fold with an ACTIVE hold issue whose lift is a string absent from fold.state.exercises (fx-gone) and an unfinished legacy DEBUT of fx-gone. Head: the registered projection hides it (projectHeld's legacy arm). Mutants: it stays (the :271 hide reads w only from exercises). The card is unaffected (pickStructural needs exById). For every held lift present in exercises the two mutants equal the head (projectHeld projects its w null and the :271 hide covers its legacy entries, done, kind and typeof alike). A hold on a lift the programme lacks needs a record naming a lift the base lacks (S1).
- S24-P26 (FC03 :266, `typeof i.lift === 'string'` dropped): input a fold with an ACTIVE hold issue whose lift is null (spec :156 "An issue whose lift cannot be resolved carries lift:null"). Head: excluded from heldProjection's lifts and issues. Mutant: lifts contains null and issues include it; the registered state changes only if an exercise id or a queue exId is null. The spec states no registered-projection effect of a lift-null hold, and both product callers read .state only.
- S24-P36 (FC03 :270, `x &&` dropped in nullW): input fold state exercises [null, ...]. Head: skips null. Mutant: TypeError reading 'w' of null.
- S24-P39 (FC03 :271, `!!q &&` dropped in hide): input fold state queue [null, ...]. Head: not hidden. Mutant: TypeError reading 'exId' of null.

- S24-H05 (L/today-bindings.mjs:666, `!rejected[o.op_id]` dropped): input two proposal-response ops carrying one issuance, the first recorded in the generation's rejected collection and the second accepted (a retry after a rejected attempt, spec :171), then an answer on the same handle. Head: alreadySaved with the ACCEPTED op_id. Mutant: may report the REJECTED op_id (first in Object.values order). Spec :172 says "report already-saved" and names no op_id.
- S24-H08 (:667, the issuance equality dropped): input two host handles for one proposal issued at different moments (same producer, body and reason, so one proposal_id; issuance.moment differs), the first accepted, then an answer on the second. Head: not found (issuance differs), re-evaluated, refused NATIVE_LOAD_STALE_OFFER. Mutant: found by proposal_id, alreadySaved with the first op_id. Spec :172 "that exact issuance/spend" does not decide whether an equal spend under another moment is already saved.
- S24-H12 (:674, `base === undefined` flipped): input a host opened on basis A (demo-press w 40), then project({base: B}) with B demo-press w 55. Head: folds from B (55). Mutant: ignores B, folds from the current engineState (40). The spec's host contract (:97 "project() -> authenticated Fold plus source basis") has no base argument; the option is FC08's own API (L/today-bindings.mjs:638-640).
- S24-H23 (:682, `revision: null`): input any successful project(). Head: revision = the repository snapshot revision. Mutant: null. Spec :97 names "authenticated Fold plus source basis" and no repository revision field.
- S24-H28 (:663, savedResponse folds `first`): input after an acknowledged yes the page's engineState function returns a basis whose exercises is not an array, then the same handle is answered again. Head: savedResponse folds with that basis, project() refuses NATIVE_LOAD_RECORD_INVALID and the answer is refused RECORD_INVALID. Mutant: folds with the basis captured at open and reports alreadySaved. For every valid basis the spend index does not depend on the basis (a genuine record keeps its spend under any base, :155-:166), and the spec does not name an invalid page basis.

### 2. Part B: method
- Surface (read only, static): FC01 rebuild/engine/native-load.cjs whole (:1-:671); FC03 rebuild/m4/workout/native-load-effects.cjs whole (:1-:1134, the export table :30-:68 skipped); the TB host section of rebuild/m3/w6/local/today-bindings.mjs (:510-:743, the native-load registrar and createNativeLoadHost; the commission's :525-:765 names the same functions, :744-:765 is the check-in lane's comment and createCheckInHost, not native-load); the SA A-LEGACY-VECTOR function of rebuild/m3/w6/local/source-admission.mjs (:850-:862, the body inside the commission's :838-:862).
- Generator gensweep.cjs (scratch, run through pm-run shared; it reads the four files as text, loads no product module): at every applicable code site, one single-clause mutant per operator: NEG (if (C) -> if (!(C)); an arrow predicate of filter/some/every/find negated), ANDOR (each && <-> ||), REL (=== <-> !==, == <-> !=, < -> <=, <= -> <, > -> >=, >= -> >), CONST (n -> n+1 and n-1; true <-> false), RET (return E -> return null), GUARD (a one-line guard removed), ARGS (the first two arguments of a same-file call swapped), FIELD (one member dropped from a returned/displayed/pushed/refused one-line object). Each `before` is widened until unique in its file; each mutant compiled in memory; 0 dropped. Overlays are applied in memory only (NLR_OVERLAY for FC01/FC03, NLR_ESMOVERLAY for TB, NLR_FSOVERLAY for SA); every TAP carries "GUARD protected-in-cache: none; refused: none" and the child's applied count.
- Generated 3192 (FC01 1192, FC03 1751, TB 209, SA 40). Over the ~600 cap, so conditions and guards first: tier 1 = NEG + GUARD (649), tier 2 = REL + ANDOR (1425). NOT RUN: CONST 428, RET 210, FIELD 290, ARGS 190 = 1118 mutants (every id listed in section 4, status N).

### 5. Part B: equivalence proofs (status E, 58; each also measured LIVE on whole FC12 and whole FA03 and on every round-27 row)
- K042: every reserve reaching nativeEffort was admitted by E/performed.cjs effort() (:25-31: undefined, a map with tag unknown/skipped/not_asked, or exact 0/1/2, at_least 3), and nativeEffort's first test already returns null for undefined/null, so a non-map value never arrives; the mutant's next test reads value.tag on a map only
- K097: FC03 is the only product caller and always builds the request as a map with lift_lineage_id/completion_op_id checked text (FC03 :961 refuses otherwise) and basis from basisOf (a map); the refusal is unreachable through the product
- K128: every op the evaluator refs through R() is in basis.coverage: FC03 basisOf covers every session op and every native op of the generation (:311), and the typed facts name only those ops; probe A2 over forged coverage (R-close-cov, Q-coverage) gives identical outputs because correspondence S5 refuses first
- K185: liftRows then filters entries by E.performedEntry, which returns null for every legacy row entry (profile not earned/performed-lift/*); a legacy row therefore adds no row either way
- K294: fit() is reached only through entryTarget on a missedQ whose newWSets came from an accepted earn (DERIVABLE: newWSets = wSets.map(+delta), every element finite, :156) or is absent; the guard's null never triggers on a reachable newWSets except a negative element, where R9.10 FIT (fit refuses x<0) and the MISSED CLOSE then refuse by name; the round-24 debt D-R13L1-3 covers negative elements (spec-silent at the missed-close compare)
- K303: FC03 always hands evaluate a state from foldNativeLoad (withFacts over a base the fold already checked Array.isArray exercises and queue, :554) or projectHeld of it
- K307: FC03 :1043 and :1011 refuse before evaluate when workoutFacts or its order is not a map; probe A2 identical
- K312: FC03 builds basis.order from the same workoutFacts it hands in (basisOf :319), so the claimed cut always equals facts.order; the only path with another basis is the fold's re-evaluation at the ORIGINAL cut, whose facts are factsAtCut(facts, b.basis.order.start_ids, b.basis.order.frontier) (:755), equal by construction; probe A2 identical
- K499: cur.start is the Start of a row liftRows built from facts, whose order.start_ids names every session Start (E/performed.cjs:168 refuses otherwise), so r >= 0 always
- K621: earnWalk candidates: newW = nextLoad (a finite positive rung, progression.cjs:359-370) and newWSets = wSets.map(+delta) (earn.cjs:63/80/88/97) over a wSets whose length equals originals (step 2 FIT GUARD :146 refused otherwise) and whose elements are finite (JSON); a non-positive element needs a vector with an element <= -delta, the D-R13L1-3 negative-load case, for which the capture already refuses the day (engine-capture :83 fit refuses x<0), so no completion reaches this check
- K645: a landed effect's frontier entry carries close_ref; its lastConsumed check (:385) cannot pass after a landing Close (the landing Close is a later row of the lift) and the capture guard in FC03 (:998 capturedAfter) refuses first; probe P-comp shows no differing output
- K679: held is defined as !q && !auth && !heldTrace, so !q && !auth && !held implies heldTrace true with no pending entry and no adopted authority: a landed, missed, compensated or superseded effect, each of which FC03 refuses first (close_ref for landed, capturedAfter for missed, the tombstone for compensated at :381, exit supersession retires q done and the fold's exit books)
- K754: the governor event is called only by FC03 (:686, :943) on a fold state whose exercises is an array (checked at :554)
- K758: FC03 calls the governor only when facts is a map (:941 if (facts)) and in sameCut with withFacts(S, cut) of a facts-derived cut; a state without workoutFacts never reaches it
- K773: governor() on a lift with no native performed opener replays nothing: updateOpenerHold is called only for rows of the lift, each with rir null when the first slot is not performed (en.rir == null is a no-op, writers.cjs:235), so hold === !!ex.holdFlag and nothing changes
- K941: FC03 calls the transition only with context.event 'accept' or 'close' (:774, :784, :927) and 'governor' (dispatched before this guard)
- K948: FC03 structural() refuses a record whose lift is absent from the base (:342 'lineage'), and every later state keeps the base's lifts (no transition removes an exercise), so ex always resolves
- K964: FC03 groups records by spend_id (:606-615) and calls the accept transition once per group; a spend already in spent at that point is only the group's own earlier coalesced record, which never re-enters
- K974: FC03 :727-:728 refuses every overlap with an existing spend before the accept transition (EFFECT_CONFLICT, the same code), so FC01's own clash refusal is reached only by a direct caller (Fable R26 FN10, the same finding)
- K1005: derivable() (:514-519) already refuses target_load unless candidate.newW equals target_load.scalar (ts !== c.newW) and FC03 folds only records whose issuance.reason passed structural() text(iss.reason) (:335); Fable R26 FN07 proof 1
- K1022: S3/S4 (correspondence) or the original-cut re-evaluation require the consumed Start's session in facts before any accept transition; an adoption whose latest evidence Start is absent from facts refuses consumes/evidence first (probe B P848)
- K1055: entryOf always records base_load from the body, and DERIVABLE (c1) requires base_load.fields.w and .wSets to be presence wrappers (:494), so f.w and f.wSets are maps for every folded spend
- K1064: compensate() reaches this branch only when heldTrace is true and no pending q exists; derivable(compensate) already refused a target naming a landed (close_ref) or cancelled spend, and FC03 refuses captured descendants first; the remaining case (an applied adoption) always has auth adopted with prior (written together at :566)
- K1076: FC03 calls the close transition only with g.body of an earn whose queue entry exists (:903-927 iterate native entries, which only earn accepts create)
- K1085: FC03 always passes completion {start: refOf(ev.start), close: refOf(close), ...} with authenticated ops (:927), so every field is present and text
- K1093: the landing loop iterates only undone native entries (:903); once landed the entry is done and never re-enters, and a second Close of the same spend is not reached
- K1107: FC03 passes q from the same state's queue with the same spend_id, id and exId (the queue entry id is the spend_id, :547)
- K1157: the landing loop runs per session event from facts.sessions (:667-671), so c.start names a session in facts
- P071: cellValue's guard returns null for a non-specified or textless cell; without it JSON.parse(undefined) throws inside the try and the catch returns null too (load.source_json undefined), and for a non-map load the property read throws before the try only when load is null/undefined, which every capture cell carries as a map (engine-capture writes load on every cell)
- P092: startCapture: a non-map cell or another lift's cell or a textless slot key then fails JSON.parse or the k[0] === lift test inside the try, giving at === null and no cell; every capture written by engine-capture names its lift and a JSON slot key
- P1090: facts.order.start_ids and facts.sessions come from the same projector (every start id has its session); the Start op is in the generation for every native session (the history projector reads it from the log)
- P1206: the fold's own overlap refusal (:728) is unreachable for host-issued records (Claude R26 D-R26C-1: same-lift groups with shared consumes are conflicted together at :651-652 before any event); only an external record could reach it and the ADMISSION GATE refuses those
- P1333: a present-revision record is re-evaluated only when its original cut is reproduced (sameCut: the programme and queue digests equal), so step 2's plan comparison at that cut reads the same plan the record was issued on and cannot refuse PLAN_CHANGED
- P133: fitVector's null return is consumed by selectedEntry (same(cap, null) false) and entryTargetOf; without the guard a malformed vector yields a vector that still differs from every capture: reachable only with a non-finite or negative newWSets element (D-R13L1-3 carried)
- P1362: the exit's adopted state comes from FC01's adoption over projectHeld (wSets null), which deletes a present-null wSets (FC01 :563, R9.11 M (3)); nx.wSets is never null here, so neither the guard nor its negation (delete of an absent key) changes the state
- P1396: the Close op of every session in facts is in the generation (the history projector reads it from the authenticated log)
- P1459: the check's fold-refused branch (:960) is reached only when foldNativeLoad returns status refused, and every refused fold is refusedFold (:550, its only producer; :554, :555, :566) with exactly ONE issue; find over a one-element array with either predicate returns that issue or falls back to issues[0], the same issue (red-first plan c1: LIVE on the rows, as the proof predicts; the row R27S-P934 was renamed so it no longer claims P1459)
- P1550: the exit branch (:1010) is reached only after the fold succeeded on the same workoutFacts; the check path's facts are the fold's (a map with order, else the fold's sessionOf finds nothing and holds.length would still apply); the host always passes the projector's facts (a map)
- P1610: every product caller passes workoutFacts from the history projector (a map with order); FC01 refuses COMPLETION_REQUIRED field workoutFacts on the same input anyway (:193)
- P1662: FC01 step 2 (b) refuses SET_COUNT_BASIS_UNPROVEN [Close Ref] field null on the same input (:237) and the check relays FC01's refusal; the FC03 guard differs only for host v1 slots, where FC01 evaluates the same state and refuses identically (N20)
- P182: sameIssued's only product caller passes a fresh FC01 offer (map) and the host-held record {proposal_id, issuance} (maps) (L/today-bindings.mjs:718)
- P199: as P092 for startPlanCapture
- P274: projectHeld with an empty set maps every exercise and filters every queue entry to themselves (set.has is false), returning an equal new object; both callers use the value only
- P292: heldProjection's callers pass a foldNativeLoad result whose state is present whenever status is ready (the registrar refuses a non-ready fold first, :527; project() guards p.fold.state, :677)
- P348: as P347: removing the revisit skip re-walks visited ancestors of an acyclic graph, same answer
- P404: the issuance arrives through strict JSON (the repository's parseStrictJson), so json(iss.body) always equals iss.body
- P590: dead code: every compensate body returns from the early compensate block (:388-:399, which ends in return null or a field), so the second compensate branch at :407-:408 is never reached; negating it changes nothing (probe A4 P-comp: identical)
- P592: as P590: the guard removed sits in the unreachable second compensate branch (:407-:408); probe A4 identical
- P766: an adopt-baseline with a non-null base vector is refused by DERIVABLE (c3) base_load (:512 adopt-baseline needs w null/ABSENT) with the same field
- P912: every product caller passes engine {revision, at} built by the host (L/today-bindings.mjs:505, :632)
- P937: an issue with lift null reaches dispute only for an unresolvable lineage; the mutant then stores it under disputed.get(null), which behindHolds never reads (it is keyed by a record's own lift, always text)
- K202: Array.isArray(ws) && ws.length > 0 vs >= 0 differ only for an empty array, where Math.min(i, ws.length - 1) = -1 gives the same k = -1 as the other branch
- K260: the sort then pop(): the two comparators differ only for two forks with an equal from, and every element they can reorder has that same from; pop() returns a fork whose from is the maximum either way and technique = String(from), so the value is the same
- K295: n <= v.length vs n < v.length differ only at n === v.length, where v.slice(0, n) and [...v, ...Array.from({ length: 0 })] are equal arrays
- P134: n <= v.length vs n < v.length differ only at n === v.length, where v.slice(0, n) and [...v, ...Array.from({ length: 0 })] are equal arrays
- P148: Array.isArray(ws) && ws.length > 0 vs >= 0 differ only for an empty array, where Math.min(i, ws.length - 1) = -1 gives the same k = -1 as the other branch
- P1672: Array.isArray(ws) && ws.length > 0 vs >= 0 differ only for an empty array, where Math.min(i, ws.length - 1) = -1 gives the same k = -1 as the other branch
- P593: the sort then pop(): the two comparators differ only for two forks with an equal from, and every element they can reorder has that same from; pop() returns a fork whose from is the maximum either way and technique = String(from), so the value is the same

### 6. Part B: SPEC-SILENT (9; no row; for the PM, each with its input and both outputs) and UNREACHABLE (4)
- K766: input: an admitted base whose exercises contains a null member (fold of F0 with exercises[0]=null, C1,C2 tops); head: ready, no issue; mutant: issue NATIVE_LOAD_EVALUATION_FAILED field governor lift null (probe D). The spec does not name a null exercise member (the round-24 S24-P05/P36 class)
- P1470: input: a check request with no lift_lineage_id over a fold with a lift-null RECORD_INVALID (a record naming lift fx-ghost); head: RECORD_INVALID field request refs []; mutant: RECORD_INVALID field lift_lineage_id refs [fx-resp-1] (probe D). Both refuse RECORD_INVALID; the spec names no field or refs for a request without a lift
- P1483: input: probe A2 N-ghost-check: L.cs (C1, C2 top) with N02c's offer re-issued as lift_lineage_id fx-ghost (absent from the base), recorded under R2; check fx-press at C2. head: fold issue RECORD_INVALID lift_lineage_id lift null refs [fx-resp-1]; check refused RECORD_INVALID field lift_lineage_id refs [fx-resp-1]. mutant (the unattributed-hold refusal removed): the same fold issue; check offers earn [105,105,105]. Spec :158 holds a lift with an active RECORD_INVALID "of its own"; the record claims fx-ghost, so fx-press has no issue of its own; "An issue whose lift cannot be resolved carries lift:null and holds back every check" is the build's comment (FC03 :572-:573), not a spec sentence (searched: no "holds back"/"lift:null" in R9.13). Held for the PM
- P428: input: as P1483 (probe A2 N-ghost-R1 / N-ghost-check). head: fold issue RECORD_INVALID lift_lineage_id lift null; check fx-press refused RECORD_INVALID lift_lineage_id refs [fx-resp-1]. mutant (the lineage test at :342 removed; S1's consumes-lift test still refuses the same field): issue lift fx-press; check fx-press refused PLAN_CHANGED refs [fx-close-2]. Spec :155 S1 names the field (lift_lineage_id, both sides) but not the issue's lift for an absent lineage; the check code then follows from the unspecified attribution. Red-first plan c7: LIVE on R27S-P426 (a genuine one-lift record passes S1 under both). Held for the PM
- P906: input: host project({base}) with a basis lacking queue (probe I); head: ok true, fold status refused (RECORD_INVALID base); mutant: project() throws reading queue.filter. The spec names no basis without a queue
- H007: input: a refused fold whose one issue is BLOCKING (e.g. CAPABILITY_REQUIRED engine or SOURCE_FRONTIER_UNPROVEN) reaching the registrar; head throws that issue's code, mutant (predicate negated) falls back to RECORD_INVALID; measured at the gym (registrar-imported probe H2) both surface WORKOUT_PREPARATION_INVALID. Spec :173 ("a refused fold refuses the new prescription by name") does not name the code the capture carries
- H022: input: createNativeLoadHost({day:'not-a-day'}); head: TypeError "createNativeLoadHost requires day"; mutant: TypeError "createGymHost requires day" (probe H2). Both throw a TypeError; only the message differs; not specified
- H026: input: createNativeLoadHost({engineState:{exercises:'none'}}); head: TypeError "createNativeLoadHost requires engineState"; mutant: TypeError "createGymHost requires engineState" (probe H2)
- H042: input: after host.close(), project() and check(); head: {ok:false, code NATIVE_LOAD_CAPABILITY_REQUIRED} and check refused CAPABILITY_REQUIRED; mutant: SESSION_CHANGED for both (probe H2). This is Claude's D-R25C-1 (carried): the spec (:97 close(), :188) names CAPABILITY_REQUIRED for an unowned handle, not project()/check() after close; held for the PM
- UNREACHABLE:
- K233: rootOf is called only on rows produced by liftRows (source 'performed') and on tenure rows whose rec is a native row in every current fold (FC03 admits no legacy baseline: the imported-generation gate refuses first, :566); the legacy branch is reachable only with an imported legacy baseline, which FC09/FC10 (unbuilt) would bring
- K522: FC01 :310 the date-mapping refusal: with the typed readers every f.tops date is a row date of V_pre, so the refusal needs a sighting date the tenure does not carry (a legacy EARNED feed line, spec :131 d); no admitted native input reaches it (legacy baselines are refused at the FC03 gate :566, FC09/FC10 unbuilt). No input, so no outputs to compare
- P1732: issuanceFor's only product caller is the host check (L/today-bindings.mjs:691), always with an FC01 offer (map body, text reason), revision engine.revision (text), source JSON.stringify(nullSource) (text) and moment clock.now() (text)
- P1747: completedLifts has no product caller (exported only)
- LIVE and UNCLASSIFIED (status L, 459; no row, no proof, no spec-silent input written; for the PM). Six tier-1 guards with a partial reading:
- P1169: probe A4/B4 identical; the draft proof (S5 refuses first) is not proven for every input. Held as unclassified
- P867: dayOf without its per-evidence return falls back to the latest session date of the cut; every probe (A4 L6, K, Q; B4) and every row gives identical output, because in every reachable cut measured the checked completion's date is the latest session date. No input found that differs; no proof that none exists (a record re-evaluated at an original cut whose latest session is a later day than its evidence would differ only in engine.at(day), whose date-scoped readers FC12 does not vary). Held for the PM as unclassified
- P878: checkedCompletion without its evidence branch falls back to the lift's latest normal completion in the cut; for every genuine earn/adoption the evidence ends at the checked completion, and the fold re-evaluates at the record's ORIGINAL cut (factsAtCut), where that completion is the lift's latest; probes A4/B4 identical. A forged record whose evidence ends earlier is refused by S3-S5 before re-evaluation. No differing input found; held as unclassified
- P953: dissolvedExit's first guard removed; probe B4 identical: for kind != adopt-baseline authority_refs is [] on every genuine record except a missed-close adopt-observed, whose single ref is a Close, so ar.every fails the proposal-response test; with holds present the later isHold test on issues rejects a ref a hold names, but a ref no hold names (RESIDUAL (iv)) would pass: not proven. Held as unclassified
- H076: the history read's source_revision vs the snapshot revision differs only when a write interleaves between the two loads (Claude D-R24-O-3, carried); FA03 has no seam for it. Not equivalent; untestable here without the seam. Held for the PM
- H198: the late already-saved return (L/today-bindings.mjs:728) is specified by :172 (lost acknowledgement, response durable), but FA03 has no lost-ack seam (Claude D-R25C-2, carried): on every write failure measured the response is absent, so late is null either way. Not equivalent; untestable here without the seam. Held for the PM
- The 453 tier-2 survivors (REL and ANDOR) by file and class; each survived whole FC12, whole FA03 where run (section 7), and every round-27 row in the loop pass. ### 0. Verdict: STOP to the PM, no product byte written

Every reason structural() returns other than 'consumed reference absent' and 'lineage' becomes RECORD_INVALID field 'payload' at FC03 :622 (outside structural()). So any structural() check refuses H-01..H-04 by field 'payload' under every revision.
- Measured (section 2): the triage's check (candidate P, below) makes every H-01..H-04 case refuse by name and never throw, but turns THREE existing rows red: R28B-S6-ORDER (basis.order null or {} under R2 is pinned to field basis.order, spec :155 S6; P gives payload), R28B-FORGED-UNDO-TARGET (an earn with target_load lacking vector, R2, pinned to field decision; P gives payload), R28B-EXIT-REFS-MISSING (the L12-B5 exit without load_basis.authority_refs, R1 and R2, pinned to field base_load; P gives payload). Brief STOP condition "any existing row to change" is met, and a fix of H-01 cannot avoid it: the H-01 input under R2 IS R28B-S6-ORDER's first case, structural() does not know the engine revision, and :622 can only say payload.

The spec's structural list (:154 "strict JSON, proposalDigest equality, authenticated membership, lineage resolution, consumed refs present") does not contain a Decision-shape check. Only H-04 (basis members with no S clause) has no spec-named field, where the head's own 'decision shape' precedent gives payload.
