(Diff file scratch fc03.diff sha256 f70d3396612d6a06174e740ab8682f60ac2b1789bc60be6719e888a9eebb0275; fc01.diff 9a26b5ae3303f301832b7d969e5061e2f5d8be2e6e61f8d1d51b557710f4091d.)
- DEPTH (the answer to D-R29F-2 as ruled, "exactly the depth the spec defines, no deeper"): exact member sets where the spec LISTS the members (accept payload :101; issuance :102 as before; Decision :104-:112; Basis :117; plan and load_basis :119; Ref :120; base_load and its FieldImage :110; the presence wrapper :156 DECODE FIRST; target_load :111; candidate :112; evidence item and set item :109; set current {load,reps,reserve} :109; coverage entry :118), containers and element types where it gives them (arrays, Refs, Loads {value,unit:lb} or {kind:configuration,configuration_key}, nullable where it says null). NOT checked, because the spec only describes them: order's and technique's member names (order needs an array of text start_ids for S6, as before), effect_frontier entries beyond {spend_id text} (the head's check), the values of plan hashes, tenure_start/sets/prefix/hi, slot/position/origin/state, current's load/reps/reserve values, candidate newW/state/t/gate/rule values, coverage disposition/source_member; each of those values is judged by S1-S8, DERIVABLE or the reproduced cut. Sortedness (coverage, effect_frontier) is not a shape and is left where it was (FC01 checks consumes order).
- FIELDS: each defect keeps the field the head already named for that member where one was named (pinned rows unchanged: R28B-BODY-NULL, R28B-ISSUANCE-EXACT payload; R29-H04-BASIS-MEMBERS payload; R28B-FORGED-UNDO-TARGET and R29-H02 decision; R28B-S4-EVIDENCE and R29-H03 evidence; R28B-S6-ORDER and R29-H01 basis.order; R28B-EXIT-REFS-MISSING and R29-H04-ADOPT-OBSERVED base_load), now R1 = R2 because the validator runs before the present-revision gate. New names only where the head threw or applied: D-R29F-4's set-level Refs are S4 evidence (the head said consumes for edits 'ab'/[5], original 5); a non-text consumes root is S1 lift_lineage_id (the head: R2 lift_lineage_id, R1 decision or issuance).
- CONTAINMENT: (i) structural() runs under try (an exception while judging a record's shape -> RECORD_INVALID payload for that record, its lift from liftOf, as any malformed accept); (ii) each fold event runs under try; `judging` names the accept group or the group whose landing is being judged; on an exception the event's partial work is undone (state, issues, spent restored to the event's start; repair/held entries of dropped issues removed) and that record is refused RECORD_INVALID payload for its own lift; an exception with no record under judgement is rethrown (none is known). The check needs no change: it folds through (i)/(ii) and reads only validated records.
- D-R29F-5 (facts null with a genuine present-revision compensation): the reproducible gate's factsAtCut still throws there, now inside (ii), so that record is refused RECORD_INVALID payload instead of the whole fold throwing; with facts null every other genuine record was already refused (S3 consumes, Fable C01). Disclosed, not a row (host reachability unproven, as Fable left it).

### 3. REVISION-MOVES (R9.13 N (ii))
- FC03 hunks: all FOLD-ONLY. recordShape and the field line run only inside foldNativeLoad's family loop; the containment sites are the fold's event loop; the seven reverted 29b hunks were fold-only (round 29b section 1). checkNativeLoad (N (ii) (a)-(e): projectHeld/basisOf over it, the authority_refs fills, missedQ, the held-path refusals, step 2) is byte-identical to bd7654a (not in the diff). No FC03 ISSUANCE hunk.
- FC01 hunk: an ISSUANCE change (the check no longer mints an adopt-observed over a configuration w), in an ENGINE file, which is what N (ii) says the revision must move for, and it moves by construction: PRODUCER_REVISION is the digest of the engine files (FC03 :1182-:1189), so FC01's new bytes give earned/native-load/v1+sha256:51730efcd11d615e88e409c664a425af4e4aa7fbcf9d7b136c29c44f939ec06c (scratch rev30.cjs reads the file bytes only; R2-REVISION recomputes the same). The constant is REBOUND in FC03, exactly as round 20 part B1 did for its FC01 clause; without it R2-REVISION is red. This is not D-R13L1-2's case (an FC03-only issuance change with no mechanism); the PM's :856 (3) anticipates the engine digest moving. Builder P FLAGGED the rebind for the PM; the PM RULED at DECISIONS:859: the rebind 1d7dbe40 -> 51730efc is NOT the STOP the brief names, it is the mechanical consequence of the FC01 change :856 ruled (:856 (3) anticipated the digest moving); D-R13L1-2's STOP is an issuance change with no mechanism. The rebind is KEPT and R2-REVISION is green on the final bytes (section 7, a-whole-fc12).
- CONSEQUENCE FOR READERS (as :859 asks): every record issued under earned/native-load/v1+sha256:1d7dbe40782cc96971a94911aa90ed9dc77a28440ef5f60fbdc088f628803def (the round-22 .. 29b engine bytes) is, at the new revision 51730efc, an ABSENT-REVISION record (spec :155 REVISION RETENTION): it is no longer re-evaluated at its original cut; it applies from its body as written only after the structural checks, S1-S8 and FC01's DERIVABLE, and raises PRODUCER_REVISION_ABSENT_APPLIED; it is never re-priced under the new code and never refused for absence alone. A NEW check that would need 1d7dbe40 refuses PRODUCER_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE. This is the same consequence every seal has (:155 "every seal changes the revision, so absence is normal").

### 6. The fuzz property R30-FUZZ-MALFORMED-RECORDS (DECISIONS:856 (1) (b))
- Generator: the record definition as a schema written in FC12 from the spec (:101 payload, :102 issuance, :104-:112 Decision, :117-:120 Basis and Ref, :61 strict JSON), used ONLY to generate violations: every (site, malformation) pair of the record at every depth the schema has, i.e. container swaps (non-iterables 5/'x'/true/{} for arrays, wrong containers, null where not nullable), type swaps, a missing member and an extra member at every exact object, a bad element appended to every array; values the spec leaves to other clauses are never mutated; basis.source is only removed. NOT generated (spec-silent D-R27-1/SS-08, post-S11 list): replacing payload, issuance, issuance.body or body.lift_lineage_id, or changing issuance.producer (the record would be unattributable or leave the family, which the build's own comment, not the spec, says holds every lift).
- Each seed picks the record kind (earn, adopt-observed 95, or the Undo of a genuine earn), R1 or R2, fx-press's record before or after fx-row's genuine yes, and one (site, malformation); the record is re-digested unless proposal_id itself is the site. Asserted per case: the fold never throws and is ready; RECORD_INVALID names the record (refs include its op) for fx-press only; nothing of it applied (w 100; an Undo's earn stays pending DEBUT 105; no spend of it, no cancellation); fx-row's exercise, queue entries, issues and spend equal the genuine-record control's; fx-press's check and fx-row's check never throw and fx-row's check equals the control's. At the default seed count every malformation class must occur.
- Defaults: NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_SEED 20300928, NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_RUNS 1000 (seeds 20300928..20301927; FC12 :7292 R30_FUZZ_RUNS=1000, R30_FUZZ_SEED=20300928): on the final bytes (a-whole-fc12, FC12 647dbe8e) 8.9 s inside a whole FC12 of 29.0 s, 410/410 green, with the property walks at their defaults (R7-PROPERTY NATIVE_LOAD_PROPERTY_RUNS 150 from seed 20260923, 2.2 s; R8-PROPERTY NATIVE_LOAD_PROPERTY8_RUNS 100 from seed 20261001, 10.5 s). Extended on the final bytes (plan h-001, finisher re-run): h-fuzz-s1-fc12 NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_RUNS=10000 NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_SEED=20310001 (seeds 20310001..20320000) green in 84.6 s; h-fuzz-s2-fc12 NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_RUNS=10000 NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ_SEED=20320001 (seeds 20320001..20330000) green in 83.1 s; union with the default: 21000 distinct seeds, no counterexample. Earlier, on the same product bytes and an FC12 whose fuzz row already had all three kinds: 10000 seeds 20310001..20320000 green in 81 s (p4).
- Red on the head: seed 20300933 (p1, rows appended in memory; a coverage entry swapped to null applied) and seed 20300930 (final FC12 bytes, head product in memory; a coverage op_id swapped to true applied).

### 2. Product: what changed, item by item (FC01 and FC03 only; no template, reason, copy or displayed string changed; no ISSUANCE change)
- (1) B-R30F-1 = B-R30C-1, FC01 derivable (c2): one line BEFORE any vector is built: `if (!Array.isArray(d.base_load.vector) || d.base_load.vector.length !== n) bad('base_load');`. (c2) already required base_load.vector to EQUAL a vector of length max(1, sets), so this is exactly (c2)'s outcome (RECORD_INVALID base_load) reached before the allocation; n is from then on bounded by the record's own (already decoded) vector. The allocation audit (section 3) found no other site sized by a record-supplied number.
- (2) L21-B1, FC03 validator: `current` is typed as :109 defines it (typed resolved load/reps/reserve or null), read with the typed performed slot's own types (E/performed.cjs recorded() and effort(), which every evidence set FC01 issues has passed): isCurrent = null, or exactly {load,reps,reserve} with load a Load (:110, the existing isLoad) or null, reps {value,unit:'rep'} with a whole count >= 0, reserve null (absent) or the typed effort ({tag} unknown/skipped/not_asked; exact 0-2 or at_least 3 in reps). Field evidence (S4 owns the evidence set).
- (3) L21-B2, FC03 correspondence S4: a non-empty PROPER prefix of the slot's edit Refs now binds current too: replayEdits replays the typed edit fold (rebuild/m4/workout/edit-history.cjs fold: corrections patch their target, {clear:true} removes a member, tombstones remove, an edit of an edit acts as edited) over EXACTLY the listed edit operations and compares the record's current with that value (field evidence). The replay binds only when it is faithful for that fact, i.e. replaying ALL the slot's edits reproduces the authentic current value (true for every history the typed fold produces; FC12's older fixtures carry payload-less correction operations, for which it is not, and they keep the round-30 behaviour). Zero edits and all edits keep their round-30 comparisons unchanged. A genuine record with a proper-prefix history stays applied (the brief's control, in R31-S4-PROPER-PREFIX-BINDS-CURRENT: w 95, never RECORD_INVALID).
- (4) L21-B3, semantic comparison: FC01 (c1) compares the two wrappers by presence and value (`sameWrapper`), not by JSON member order; FC03 movedBase (:156 UNPROVABLE ORDER) compares the recorded wrapper with the image the same way (`sameImage`; without it the (c1)-accepted record was held EFFECT_CONFLICT load_basis); and the present-revision original-cut reproduction, which is :61's own comparison ("semantic equality compares the validated full data"), compares the re-evaluated body with the record key-order-insensitively (`sameData` = JSON of the recursively key-sorted data; array order stays data). Without the third change the R1 half of L21-B3 was refused RECORD_INVALID issuance (a02, first run: 'base_load.fields.w fx-revision-1 ... issuance'); it is disclosed as the one site added to the brief's named (c1) site (section 11, D-R31-1).
- (5) L21-B4 / D-R30C-2, FC03 containment: complete. The round-30 catch undid only the event's `state`, the issue and spend array lengths and the repair/held books; in-place writes to earlier entries (a spend's close_ref) and the record's EARLIER effects (its queued entry when its landing threw) stayed. Now an unexpected exception while judging a record's accept or landing throws `Contained(that record's op ids)` out of foldOnce, and foldNativeLoad replays the fold with that record refused BEFORE anything applies (RECORD_INVALID, field payload, refs = all its records, its own lift), exactly as a malformed accept: nothing of it is applied anywhere, and every other lift folds as without it. Each replay refuses at least one more record (the loop guard rethrows otherwise), so at most one replay per record; no non-injected input reaches the catch (section 7, C01). R30-CONTAINMENT's landing assertion pinned the state :155 forbids and is CORRECTED (section 5).
- REVISION: FC01 changed, so the engine digest moved 51730efcd11d615e88e409c664a425af4e4aa7fbcf9d7b136c29c44f939ec06c -> 980b80cdf0a480342a8df0c5b7ba6d4323ee04b70b4ba3d03b18f7881c066607 (rev31.cjs, the R2-REVISION recomputation from the bytes, static); PRODUCER_REVISION rebound (FC03 last hunk); R2-REVISION green in every whole FC12 run. ### 3. The allocation audit (brief item (1)): every loop or allocation in FC01/FC03 and what sizes it (line numbers on the final bytes)
Record-supplied numbers in a v1 record: load_basis.sets, prefix, hi, tenure_start; the inc and steps wrapper values; every Load value; candidate.newW and newWSets entries; an evidence set's position; basis.order.frontier. Record-supplied ARRAYS (evidence, sets, edits, coverage, consumes, authority_refs, effect_frontier, vectors, steps, newWSets) are already decoded input: a loop over them is bounded by the record's own size, never by a number in it.
| site | sized by | bound |
|---|---|---|
| FC01:492 derivable wantVector, Array.from length n and planVector({sets: n}) | n = max(1, load_basis.sets) (RECORD) | THE one record-number site: FC01:490 refuses base_load unless base_load.vector.length === n, BEFORE either allocation (round 31) |
| FC01:110 planVector, FC01:129 baseLoad, FC01:398 compensation | ex.sets of the admitted state (base programme; no native writer writes sets) | not a record number |
| FC01:406 compensation RESTORE | n0 = original slot count of the consumed completion (authentic facts) | not a record number |
| FC01:178 entryTarget, FC01:185 fit (n - v.length) | count/n = captured original slot count (authentic); v = newWSets (a record ARRAY) | not a record number |
| FC01:519/:524 E.nextLoad/E.loadRungs over the record's inc and steps | steps.map/filter/sort over the record's steps ARRAY; inc only in +(w + inc).toFixed(2) | no loop sized by a number |
| FC01:527 wSets.map; validDecision every/sort; evidenceOf maps | record ARRAYS | input-sized |
| FC03:143 entryTargetOf, FC03:149 fitVector, FC03:159 projectBase | cap.length (the Start's authentic capture) | not a record number |
| FC03:1211 checkNativeLoad planNow | ex.sets of the fold state (base programme) | not a record number |
| FC03:48 sha256Hex | the JSON length of the record | input-sized |
| FC03:27 canon (round 31) | recursion over the record body | input-sized (the body already passed strict JSON) |
| FC03:508-:509 S4 set loop | item.sets.length, refused unless equal to the authentic slot count BEFORE the loop | authentic |
| FC03:451 replayEdits (round 31) | the listed edit ids: the fact's authentic edit_op_ids or their prefix (edits.length <= now.length is checked first) | authentic |
| FC03:297 provenBefore stack | the authenticated op graph | authentic |
| FC03:632 checkedCompletion, dayOf loops | facts.order.start_ids / the record's evidence ARRAY | authentic / input-sized |
| FC03:651 foldNativeLoad replay (round 31) | contained records | at most one pass per record plus one |
prefix, hi, tenure_start, position and order.frontier size nothing (FC01 never reads prefix/hi/tenure_start; position and frontier are only compared). ### 11. STOPs, disclosures and named debts
STOP conditions checked, none met: no template, reason, copy or displayed string changed (FC01's two hunks add a comparison helper, a refusal line and comments; FC03's hunks add validator predicates, the replay, the containment replay and the reproduction comparison; no string a user sees was touched); no ISSUANCE semantics change (evaluate/earn/compensation/issuance bytes untouched; the FC01 changes are in derivable, which judges records); no existing row went red except the one authorized R30-CONTAINMENT correction (a01: 409/410 with exactly that assertion); nothing outside FC01 and FC03 in product (git status: the five round-30 files plus untracked reviews; FA03 byte-unchanged).
Disclosures:
- D-R31-1 (the one site beyond the brief's named (c1) site): L21-B3's R1 half was refused RECORD_INVALID issuance by the present-revision reproduction comparison (same(o.body, body)), which is :61's own "semantic equality ... of the validated full data". It now compares key-order-insensitively (sameData); movedBase's wrapper comparison (UNPROVABLE ORDER) had to follow (c1) too. Other member-order-sensitive comparisons remain, all refusing only a re-ordered record (the host writes one order): FC01 (c2) Load compares (a base_load Load written {unit,value}), FC01 compensate target compares, FC03 S4 current compares (a current written {reps,load,reserve}) and the MISSED-DEBUT claim's wrapper/Load compares. A PM word on whether "member order never decides" reaches Loads and current is owed; not changed here (the brief's "smallest change"). The a02 TAP that showed the R1 'issuance' refusal was overwritten by a02's re-run (same tag); the message is quoted from the session.
- D-R31-2: the proper-prefix binding binds only a FAITHFUL history (replaying all the slot's edits reproduces its authentic current). Every history the typed edit fold produces is faithful; FC12's older fixtures carry payload-less correction operations and keep the round-30 behaviour, which is why R31-CURRENT-TYPED-LEAVES pins the typing on such a history separately.
- D-R31-3: O22 LIVE (a correction of a correction inside a proper prefix): no fixture; the typed fold's admission of that shape is not established here.
- D-R31-4: containment now replays the whole fold once per contained record (bounded; unreached by any non-injected input: C01); a record refused by containment holds its lift from its own place in the log like any RECORD_INVALID record (log-ordered hold). D-R30F-1's genuine Undo under workoutFacts null is still refused RECORD_INVALID payload (now by the replay).
- D-R31-5: R31-FUZZ is a new row; R30-FUZZ is unchanged (its seeds still kill V03/V04/V06/V07/V17/V18/V20 and RC-02..05). The allocation bound is a test-only wrapper around Array.from during the folds of R31-SETS and R31-FUZZ.
- D-R31-6: O20 = D-R30C-5 carried (a contained compensation group's alt Refs are unpinned; unreachable).
- Carried unchanged: D-R30F-1, D-R30F-2 = D-R30C-1 (pre-S1 field names, post-S11 spec list per DECISIONS:861), D-R30F-3, D-R30F-4/D-R30C-3/D-L21-OPAQUE (spec-silent values; R31-FUZZ now pins "refused or applied as genuine" for prefix, hi, tenure_start, order.frontier), D-R30F-5/D-R30C-4 (today-entry UI policy: builder U), D-R30F-8 (holes/-0), D-R30C-6, D-L21-N09 (its clause is gone: nothing to clean), D-L21-N14, and every earlier carried debt.
- Process: the plans a01, a02, a03 and r01..r04 ran through go31.ps1 (one detached pm-run shared call each), the queue through two workers (worker31.ps1, one pm-run shared call per plan); the static tools rev31, mk31, aud31, ranc31, mkown31 ran as single short shared jobs while at most one plan ran, so builder P never held more than two shared slots. The workers were stopped twice (taskkill of their process trees) to extend a row and re-queued from the start; their dead pm-run locks were cleared by pm-run's stale-lock rule. A first split of the whole re-run plan (l01a/l01b) was malformed (one 'System.Object[]' line each; nothing ran) and rebuilt as l02a/l02b. The PC's bridge dropped three times for a few minutes; the detached workers ran on. Scratch plan and cmd files were written with [IO.File]::WriteAllText (UTF-8, no BOM), never Set-Content; worktree files only with write_file/edit_block. Protected five, src/, ledgers, private, soak and prepledger-dev paths: never read, listed, loaded or run (every TAP guard line 'protected-in-cache: none; refused: none'). Nothing committed, staged or pushed; DECISIONS/STATUS untouched; nothing written in nlr-r30-scratch.

diff --git "a/C:\\Users\\joeym\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\nlr-r31-scratch\\fc01-before.cjs" b/rebuild/engine/native-load.cjs

+++ b/rebuild/engine/native-load.cjs
@@ -470,7 +470,10 @@ function derivable(d, spent, refs, ex) {
   const lb0 = map(d.basis) && map(d.basis.load_basis) ? d.basis.load_basis : {};
   const fields = map(d.base_load.fields) ? d.base_load.fields : {};
   const wrapped = (x) => map(x) && typeof x.present === 'boolean';
-  if (!wrapped(lb0.w) || !wrapped(fields.w) || !same(lb0.w, fields.w) || !wrapped(lb0.wSets) || !wrapped(fields.wSets) || !same(lb0.wSets, fields.wSets)) bad('base_load');
+  // Spec :61 ("semantic equality compares the validated full data"; round 31, Astra L21-B3): two wrappers are equal when their
+  // presence and value are, whatever their member order.
+  const sameWrapper = (a, b) => a.present === b.present && same(a.value, b.value);
+  if (!wrapped(lb0.w) || !wrapped(fields.w) || !sameWrapper(lb0.w, fields.w) || !wrapped(lb0.wSets) || !wrapped(fields.wSets) || !sameWrapper(lb0.wSets, fields.wSets)) bad('base_load');
   const w = dec(lb0.w), wSets = dec(lb0.wSets), inc = dec(lb0.inc), steps = dec(lb0.steps);
   // SUPPORTED wSets: ABSENT, present-null (the scalar repeats, as planVector does) or an array.
   if (!(wSets === ABSENT || wSets === null || Array.isArray(wSets))) bad('load_basis.wSets');
@@ -481,6 +484,10 @@ function derivable(d, spent, refs, ex) {
   const noW = w === ABSENT || w === null;
   if (!noW && typeof w !== 'number' && typeof w !== 'string') bad('base_load');
   const n = Math.max(1, (Number.isSafeInteger(lb0.sets) ? lb0.sets : 0) || 1);
+  // Round 31 (Fable B-R30F-1 = Claude B-R30C-1): the record's own sets never sizes an allocation before it is checked. (c2) needs
+  // base_load.vector of length max(1, sets), so a record whose vector has another length is refused base_load here, BEFORE any
+  // vector is built; n is then bounded by the record's own vector.
+  if (!Array.isArray(d.base_load.vector) || d.base_load.vector.length !== n) bad('base_load');
   const wantScalar = noW ? null : loadOf(w);
   const wantVector = noW ? Array.from({ length: n }, () => null) : planVector({ w, sets: n, ...(Array.isArray(wSets) ? { wSets } : {}) }).map(loadOf);
   if (!same(d.base_load.scalar, wantScalar) || !same(d.base_load.vector, wantVector)) bad('base_load');
```

 const json = (x) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
 const same = (a, b) => JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
 const map = (x) => x !== null && typeof x === 'object' && !Array.isArray(x);
+// Spec :61 "semantic equality compares the validated full data" (round 31, Astra L21-B3): the same data whatever the member order

+const canon = (x) => (Array.isArray(x) ? x.map(canon) : map(x) ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(x).sort().map((k) => [k, canon(x[k])])) : x);

   const forks = map(body.basis) && map(body.basis.technique) && Array.isArray(body.basis.technique.forks) ? body.basis.technique.forks : [];

+  const sameImage = (a, b) => map(b) && a.present === b.present && same(a.value, b.value);
+  return !sameImage(img('w'), f.w) || !sameImage(img('wSets'), f.wSets) || !same(json(ex.forks || []), forks);
 }
 // Spec R9.2 :158: the named refusals that hold a lift (earns refused, the adoption exit open).
 const HOLD_CODES = new Set(['NATIVE_LOAD_RECORD_INVALID', 'NATIVE_LOAD_EFFECT_CONFLICT', 'NATIVE_LOAD_SOURCE_OVERLAP', 'NATIVE_LOAD_BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED']);
@@ -364,6 +371,14 @@ const isRef = (r) => has(r, ['op_id', 'commitment']) && text(r.op_id) && text(r.
 const isLoad = (x) => x === null || (has(x, ['value', 'unit']) && x.unit === 'lb' && typeof x.value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(x.value)) ||
   (has(x, ['kind', 'configuration_key']) && x.kind === 'configuration' && text(x.configuration_key));
 const isWrapper = (x) => has(x, ['present', 'value']) && typeof x.present === 'boolean' && (x.present || x.value === null);
+// Round 31 (Astra L21-B1): :109 "current is typed resolved load/reps/reserve or null", typed as the typed performed slot types them
+// (E/performed.cjs recorded() and effort(), which every evidence set passed when FC01 issued it): load a Load (:110) or null, reps
+// {value,unit:'rep'} with a whole count, reserve null (absent) or the typed effort: {tag} unknown/skipped/not_asked, exact 0-2 or
+// at_least 3 in reps.

### 2. Product: what changed, item by item (FC01 and FC03 only; no template, reason, copy or displayed string changed; no ISSUANCE change)
- (1) SEMANTIC EQUALITY, ONE MECHANISM (spec :61, :156; D-R31-1 = D-R31F-1 = D-R31C-1 = L22-B1, B-R31C-2, L22-B6): each module's single data comparator `same` is now `JSON.stringify(canon(a)) === JSON.stringify(canon(b))`, `canon` being round 31's recursive member sort (arrays keep their order: array order is data). FC03's separate `sameData` is gone; its two call sites (the present-revision reproduction compares) call `same`. FC01 carries the identical `canon` (the engine module cannot require FC03). Every comparison of data in both files goes through `same` (section 3 lists each site), so no comparison depends on member order. The checks that must see the record as received run first and are unchanged in what they detect: the strict-JSON check (FC03:346, the record against its own JSON copy), the negative-zero check (item 2) and the proposal digest (FC03:349, a byte digest by definition). No record body is rewritten: the fold applies and outputs the record's own objects as before (effects carry base_load/target_load as written); only the comparisons are order-free.
- (2) STRICT JSON, NEGATIVE ZERO (spec :61 "no negative zero"; L22-B3): FC03 `negZero(x)` (Object.is(x, -0), recursively through arrays and plain objects) is checked inside the ONE record validator, clause by clause, on the raw issuance body (never the strict-JSON copy, which prints -0 as 0), so a -0 anywhere in the record is refused RECORD_INVALID with the field of the clause that owns the member: evidence (S4), basis.coverage (S5), basis.order (S6), basis.source (S7), load_basis and base_load ('base_load', S8 / DERIVABLE (c1)-(c2)), candidate, target_load, and 'payload' for the Basis members no S clause names (athlete_id, plan, technique, effect_frontier). The Decision's own members and the envelope/issuance members are already required to be text or null, so no number can sit there. No genuine record carries -0 (FC01 returns json(offers); the host stores JSON).
- (3) COUNTS (L22-B4, D-R31C-2): FC01 derivable no longer coerces load_basis.sets: it must be null (or absent: no programme count) or a non-negative safe integer, else RECORD_INVALID base_load, exactly as (c2) refuses a vector of another length; then n = max(1, sets || 1) (:156 max(1, sets)). Before, any non-safe-integer (2.5, 0.5, 1e20, 1e308, 2^53, '1', true) was read as one set, and -1 as one set. Audit of every other record-supplied count: an evidence set's position is compared with the authentic slot's by === (never coerced); reps and reserve values are typed by isReps/isReserve (whole, exact); prefix, hi and tenure_start are never read by FC01/FC03 (spec-silent values, D-R30F-4/D-R30C-3; R31-FUZZ pins "refused or applied as genuine"); basis.order.frontier is compared by !== with the authentic frontier (a number); FieldImage values such as topRun are verbatim images (:111), compared and copied, never read as a count. No count is coerced anywhere.
- (4) S4 FROM THE AUTHENTIC FACT (B-R31C-1 = L22-B2): the proper-prefix clause (FC03:540) compares the record's current with `upTo.active ? cur(upTo.value) : null`, the authentic fact after exactly the listed edits (its value when active, null when removed), never the record's own claimed state (round 31 used `set.state === 'performed' ? ... : null`, so a set claimed skipped or removed with current null passed whatever the fact was). Refusal field evidence.
- REVISION: FC01 changed, so the engine digest moved 980b80cdf0a480342a8df0c5b7ba6d4323ee04b70b4ba3d03b18f7881c066607 -> df0b960424b5f0c0a7b797cd198ddd4f1643f3e4ade9c9dd96a1a86613b40ee5 (rev32.cjs: R2-REVISION's recomputation from the bytes, static); PRODUCER_REVISION rebound (FC03 last hunk); R2-REVISION green in every whole FC12 run. REVISION-MOVES consequence (:155; mechanical per DECISIONS:859): a record issued under 980b80cd is now absent-revision, applied as written after S1-S8 and DERIVABLE with PRODUCER_REVISION_ABSENT_APPLIED, never refused for absence; a NEW check needing 980b80cd is PRODUCER_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE. No shipped installation carries such records.
- ISSUANCE unchanged, measured by reading: FC01 evaluate/earn/compensation compare only arrays of ids, dates and numbers or objects FC01 builds itself member by member (refsOf), where the canonical and the byte comparison give the same answer for every input; evaluate, earn, compensation, issuanceFor and every reason string are byte-identical.

### 3. Every comparison site in FC01 and FC03 and how it is now order-free (line numbers on the final bytes)
One mechanism: `same(a, b)` = JSON of the recursively member-sorted data (FC01:26-27, FC03:28-29). "order-free by type" means the compared values are strings, numbers, null or arrays of them (no members to order). Each "same" site that compares objects is pinned by a site mutant (section 7) turned red by a round-32 row.

Every other comparison in both files is === / !== / includes on strings, numbers, op ids and spend ids (never on objects); replayEdits applies a patch member by member (a patch's member order cannot change its result).

### 5. FUZZ extension (R32-FUZZ), property walks and shards
R32-FUZZ is a NEW row beside R30-FUZZ and R31-FUZZ (both unchanged). Seeds NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ32_SEED (default 20320929), runs NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ32_RUNS (default 800); each seed draws one of four parts:
- order: one of R31-FUZZ's six record kinds (earn, adopt-observed, RETIRE Undo, RESTORE Undo, adopt-baseline, exit (a)), R1 or R2, before or after fx-row's genuine yes, with EVERY object of the accept record (payload, issuance, body and everything inside) in a seeded random member order, re-digested -> the press projection, spends, issues and both lifts' checks equal the genuine control exactly.
- zero: the same kinds with ONE numeric leaf of the body (every leaf a candidate: evidence positions, current values, base_load and FieldImage values, load_basis counts and wrappers, target_load, candidate, order.frontier, source.W) set to -0 (re-digested) -> exactly one RECORD_INVALID for the record, field = the owning clause by an independent test-side map (r32owner), nothing applied, fx-row unaffected.
- history: a generated edit history of one C2 set (2-4 edits: reps corrections, reserve set, reserve CLEARED, a correction REMOVED by a tombstone, a CORRECTION OF A CORRECTION), each prefix interpreted by the REAL typed reader (a history it does not admit is skipped and counted); the yes after a random proper prefix; the genuine record applies (w 95, never RECORD_INVALID); the record with that set's current from another prefix, reps + 1, or claimed skipped/removed with current null -> RECORD_INVALID evidence, nothing applied.
- count: the one-slot fixture (sets 1) with load_basis.sets a typed count (null, 0, 1: applied exactly as genuine) or not (2, 3, -1, -(2^53), 2.5, 0.5, 1e20, 1e308, 2^53, 2^53+2, '1', true, false, {}, []: RECORD_INVALID base_load, nothing applied), under the allocation bound.
At the default seeds every part, kind, owner (evidence, base_load, target_load, basis.source, basis.order, candidate), history class, forgery and count class is generated (asserted); the row prints its counts as a TAP diagnostic. Defaults (seeds 20320929..20321728): order 185, zero 211, history 197 (6 not admitted by the reader), count 207; owners evidence 63, base_load 90, target_load 40, source 9, order 8, candidate 1; history classes clear 71, removed correction 68, correction of a correction 71, reserve 56; forgeries another prefix 93, reps 47, state claim 51; counts typed 67, bad 140.
Runs on the final bytes (every TAP 'GUARD protected-in-cache: none; refused: none'): defaults inside whole FC12 z-whole-fc12 (R32-FUZZ 6.3 s, R31-FUZZ 13.1 s, R30-FUZZ 10.0 s, R7 2.4 s, R8 11.4 s; whole FC12 52.3 s, under the brief's two minutes). Extended shards on the final FC12 (plan y01): R32-FUZZ 10000 seeds from 20350001 (76 s; history 2492 of which 65 not admitted, correction of a correction 958, removed correction 950, clear 919) and 20360001 (77 s); R31-FUZZ 10000 from 20370001 (123 s) and 20380001 (123 s); R30-FUZZ 10000 from 20390001 (97 s) and 20400001 (98 s); R7 walk 1500 from 20330101 and 20340101 (24 s each); R8 walk 1000 from 20330201 (103 s) and 20340201 (100 s): all 1/1 green. ### 10. STOPs, disclosures and named debts
STOP conditions checked, none met: no template, reason, copy or displayed string changed (FC01's hunks add the comparator, one refusal line and comments; FC03's add the comparator, the negZero clauses, the S4 expected value and comments, and rebind the revision); no ISSUANCE semantics change (evaluate, earn, compensation, issuanceFor and every reason are byte-identical; their comparisons are over ids, dates, numbers or objects FC01 builds itself, where the semantic comparator answers as the byte one did); no existing row went red and none was edited (a01 on the first draft: FC12 431/431 incl. the 11 new rows, FA03 103/103; FC12 numstat against its start 363/0); nothing outside FC01 and FC03 in product (git status: the five round-31 files plus untracked reviews; FA03 byte-unchanged).
Disclosures:
- D-R32-1 (rows grew during the queue): R32-DR31F2 gained 'reserve at_least 4' at 03:47 (Claude K18 LIVE on its screen) and 'reps in unit lb' and 'reserve {tag:unknown} with an extra member' at 03:55 (Astra M01, M05 LIVE on their screens); R32-L22B1, -BR31C2 and -L22B3 gained their later cases before the queue started (03:15-03:17, red first on the head in r02). Lines screened before 03:55 ran on an FC12 whose R32-DR31F2 had fewer cases; rows only GAIN assertions, so a kill stays a kill, and every LIVE line was re-run whole on the final bytes (w-phase), where K18, M01 and M05 are KILLED. z01 and the y01 shards ran on the final FC12 (faa5ea4b).
- D-R32-2 (PC reboot): the PC rebooted at 06:46 (LastBootUpTime) during the w-phase; the bridge was down about 06:28-07:22. The worktree bytes were re-verified unchanged afterwards; pm-run cleared the three stale slot locks by its own rule; the 25 FC12 w-lines not yet done were re-run (w11-w13, 07:23-07:31). The FA03 w-lines were not re-run: FA03 has no walk rows, so their screens were already whole runs.
- D-R32-3 (counts): -1 is now refused base_load (the brief's "non-negative safe integer", DECISIONS:863 (3)), although (c2)'s formula max(1, sets) alone would read it as one set; sets 0 and null (and an absent member, reachable only by a direct FC01 caller, since the FC03 validator requires the member) stay admitted as the spec types them (max(1, sets) = 1). prefix, hi and tenure_start are read by no clause (spec-silent values, D-R30F-4 / D-R30C-3, post-S11 list) and are therefore never coerced; their types are not newly judged.
- D-R32-4 (strict JSON): the strict-JSON check (FC03:346) now compares the record with its JSON copy through the semantic comparator; on plain data it detects exactly what it did (P48 EQUIVALENT); on a non-plain object (a Date or a toJSON member) it is stricter and refuses where the byte check passed. Records are parsed JSON, so no genuine record is affected.
- D-R32-5: R32-FUZZ's history part takes its oracle from the real typed reader (edit-history.cjs, loaded read-only by FC12; it loads no protected module: every TAP's guard line is 'protected-in-cache: none'); histories the reader does not admit are skipped and counted (6 of 197 at defaults, 65 and 55 of about 2490 in the shards).
- Process: every run went through node %TEMP%\pm-run.cjs shared (go32.ps1 for single plans; three queue workers worker32.ps1, one pm-run shared call per plan, never more than three shared slots; static tools rev32, anc32, show32, mk32 as short shared jobs while at most two plans ran). Worktree files were written only with write_file/edit_block (LF, ASCII). Scratch plan and cmd files were written by the inherited kit with [IO.File]::WriteAllText (UTF-8, no BOM), and one scratch cmd file (s-show32.cmd) with Out-File -Encoding ascii; never Set-Content. Protected five, src/, ledgers, private, soak and prepledger-dev paths: never read, listed, loaded or run (every TAP guard line 'protected-in-cache: none; refused: none'). Nothing committed, staged or pushed; DECISIONS/STATUS untouched; nothing written in nlr-r31-scratch or any earlier scratch (its plans and overlays were only read).
Named debts carried (not changed this round): D-R31C-3 (K08, the faithfulness gate), D-R30C-5 = O20 = K10 (a contained group's alt refs), D-R31C-4 (a throwing session fact after `judging` is charged to the landed record; unreached), D-R31C-5 and builder U's S1-S4 (post-S11 list, DECISIONS:863), D-R31F-3 (basis.order.start_ids order, spec-silent), D-R30F-1 (a genuine Undo under workoutFacts null refused by containment), D-R30C-1 / D-R30F-2 (pre-S1 field names), D-R30F-4 / D-R30C-3 / D-L21-OPAQUE, D-L21-N14, D-L22-TE-CONTEXT, D-L22-TE-POLICY, D-L22-N09, D-L22-RANGES and every earlier carried debt. Paid this round: D-R31-1 = D-R31F-1 = D-R31C-1 = L22-B1 (every site, section 3), B-R31C-2, L22-B6, L22-B3, L22-B4 = D-R31C-2, B-R31C-1 = L22-B2, B-R31C-3, L22-B5, L22-B7 (= D-R31-3, O22 now KILLED), D-R31F-2 and D-L22-TYPED-DOMAIN (M01, M03, M04, M05 KILLED), D-L22-RAW-TUPLE (the authentic-Load case is now order-free and pinned).

### 2. Product: what changed, item by item (FC01 and FC03 only; no template, reason, copy or displayed string changed; the ONE issuance change is the authorized L23-B5 item)

- (1) L23-B1 = D-R32C-2, S4 binds the typed state (spec :109 "position/origin/state are the typed slot's fields", :155 S4). FC03 `correspondence` S4 now binds each evidence set's `state` to the authentic fact after exactly the listed edits, in the same places it binds `current`: every edit listed -> the typed slot's own state (a removed fact named among `removed_facts` -> `removed`); no edit listed of a fact that has edits -> `performed` (its original fact); a non-empty proper prefix -> `performed` when the replayed fact is active after exactly those edits, else `removed` (checked beside the round-32 current check, under the same faithfulness gate). A set that names no fact and no skip (original null) is bound to a state that names neither: `unlogged`, `unresolved` or `removed` (NO_FACT_STATES; `removed` with no fact named is the typed shape when several removed facts leave none to name, and the round-32 form, below). A false state claim is RECORD_INVALID field `evidence`, original and added sets alike. S8 now reads the actual loads from the bound `current` alone (`map(s.current) && map(s.current.load)`), never a claimed state (spec :155 "Actual loads here are the values S4 binds"): with S4's binding, state `performed` and a non-null current coincide wherever either is bound, so no genuine record changes outcome; a relabelled original set is now refused `evidence` by S4 instead of `target_load` / `base_load` by S8 (Claude P3, Fable S01-S03).
- (2) L23-B2, counts (spec :119; E/performed.cjs:168 admits order.frontier only as a non-negative safe integer; DECISIONS:865 (2)). FC03 `recordShape` refuses, never coerces: `basis.order.frontier` not a non-negative safe integer -> field `basis.order` (its own line after the order clause); `load_basis.prefix` not null and not a non-negative safe integer -> field `base_load` (its own line after the base_load clause; null is what basisOf writes when the programme has no count). load_basis.sets stays as round 32 made it (FC01 DERIVABLE, field base_load). Audit of the other record numbers: evidence `position` is compared `===` with the typed slot's (bound, S4); `load_basis.hi` and `tenure_start` are not typed as counts by the spec (:119 lists them; hi is the programme's own value, which :233 keys only when it is a positive safe integer, and basisOf writes tenure_start null), so they are left to their clauses (D-R33-2). -0 at prefix/frontier is still refused by the round-32 negative-zero clauses (Number.isSafeInteger(-0) is true), which R33-L23B3 now pins.
- (3) L23-B5 = D-R32C-1 (ISSUANCE change authorized for this item only, DECISIONS:865 (3); spec :109 "original is its fact/skip Ref or null when absent; edits is ordered [Ref]", :155 D-R9-1, :166). FC01 `evidenceOf`: `const f = slot.fact || removedFact(slot)`, where `removedFact` returns the slot's ONE removed fact only when the slot has no live fact, its state is `removed` and `removed_facts` has exactly one entry; that set is then recorded with the fact's Ref as `original` and its edit Refs as `edits` (current stays null: the slot is not performed). Every other slot, and every other issuance output, is unchanged (section 5 shows it byte for byte over the fixtures). The fold keeps the consent with the repair notice for the later edit: after the removal is undone the named fact is the slot's live fact again, S4 binds the listed prefix (removed, current null) through the existing proper-prefix replay, and `evidenceChanged` raises BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED (spec :166) with the yes applied. FC03 `evidenceChanged` mirrors FC01's new form and still accepts the round-32 form of the same unchanged slot (original null, no edits), so a record the round-32 producer issued for such a slot is not newly marked BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED (pinned by R33-ROUND32-REMOVED-FORM-STAYS-HISTORY). Not changed (D-R33-1): FC03 S4 still prefers the slot's live fact over a removed fact the record names, so Claude's P1 (the consumed slot re-logged with a NEW fact after a removal after the yes) is still RECORD_INVALID.
- REVISION-MOVES (:859, mechanical): the FC01 bytes moved, so PRODUCER_REVISION moves earned/native-load/v1+sha256:df0b960424b5f0c0a7b797cd198ddd4f1643f3e4ade9c9dd96a1a86613b40ee5 -> earned/native-load/v1+sha256:3c86253710607d45b467047ec858b8780483643cd5c3b9b53306040a59099b5e (rev33.cjs, R2-REVISION's recipe over the 14 engine files; only native-load.cjs moved); R2-REVISION green on the final bytes. Records of the round-32 revision are now absent-revision records (applied after S1-S8 and DERIVABLE with PRODUCER_REVISION_ABSENT_APPLIED, :155).

### 4. FUZZ extension (R33-FUZZ), property walks and shards

- R33-FUZZ-ZERO-MEMBERS-REMOVED-SETS-COUNTS (env NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ33_RUNS / NATIVE_LOAD_FUZZ33_SEED, default 1000 / 20330929; R30-, R31- and R32-FUZZ and their seeds unchanged). Three parts. ZERO: -0 at a numeric member the record's schema ALLOWS, not only an existing numeric leaf: an existing numeric leaf, a null member (a nullable count, a FieldImage value, an origin, a close_ref, a source member, a null candidate/compensates), a FRESH member of an object the spec leaves open (basis.technique and its forks, basis.source, an effect_frontier item, basis.plan, a FieldImage value object) or a fresh element of such an array (fork_refs, forks, effect_frontier, a FieldImage value array); the owning clause is drawn first (each equally likely), then a site; asserted: exactly one RECORD_INVALID with the owning clause's field (r32owner; a null Decision member is the Decision's own clause), nothing applied, fx-row and its check unchanged, under the allocation bound. HISTORY: an ADDED set's history read by the REAL typed reader (edit-history.cjs normalizeWorkoutHistory, read-only), with the set itself REMOVED and RESTORED (a tombstone of the set, a tombstone of that tombstone), corrections, a cleared reserve and a correction of a correction; the check at a random prefix (the set active or removed then; the typed slot shaped as the projector shapes it, `fact` or `removed_facts`); asserted: the offer's evidence IS the typed slot at the yes (:109: fact Ref, the reader's effect ids as edits, state, current), the genuine record folded after the whole history applies (w 95, spent) with BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED iff a later edit exists, and the record re-issued with another state (current kept), another prefix's current or other reps is RECORD_INVALID evidence. COUNT: the one-slot fixture with load_basis.sets, load_basis.prefix or basis.order.frontier a count the spec types (applied exactly as the genuine record) or not (-1, -(2^53), 2.5, 0.5, 1e20, 1e308, 2^53, 2^53+2, '1', true, false, {}, []; plus 2 and 3 for the one-slot sets) -> RECORD_INVALID base_load / basis.order.

### 5. Issuance before/after comparison (the L23-B5 issuance change only; DECISIONS:865 (3) "prove it with a before/after comparison over the fixtures")

Method: preload iss33.cjs (scratch only, never in the worktree): when the worktree FC01 is compiled its factory is wrapped, so EVERY `evaluateNativeLoad(state, request)` call made anywhere in the fixtures (the checks of every row, the fold's present-revision re-evaluations, the walks and the four fuzzes at defaults, the FA03 host) runs the NEW FC01 (whose result the tests receive) AND the round-32 FC01 (fc01-head33.cjs, e589d0fc79e7f63e98d87fa48e25b6265ef9e44ec9877940f0266814e27d7f75, built with the same injected engine table) on the same input, and compares the two results as JSON text (status, the echoed basis, every offer's body and reason, the refusal). Each differing call is classified by whether its input state holds a slot whose one fact is removed (no live fact, state removed, removed_facts). Final bytes (plan w2, c02-fc12 441/441 with the preload, c02-fa03 103/103):

| Run | evaluate calls | offers | byte-equal | of them with a removed-only slot | differ | differ WITH a removed-only slot | differ WITHOUT |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| whole FC12 (441 rows, walks and fuzzes at defaults) | 17919 | 14121 | 17789 | 1 | 130 | 130 | 0 |
| whole FA03 (103 cells, the real host) | 1511 | 1336 | 1511 | 33 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

Every issuance output of every input without a removed-only slot is byte-identical to the round-32 producer's (missed-debut, adopt-baseline, adopt-observed, earn and compensation offers and every refusal included); the only outputs that moved are offers whose evidence covers a slot whose one fact is removed (R33-L23B5, R33-ROUND32-REMOVED-FORM-STAYS-HISTORY, R33-STATE-OF-... (a), R33-FUZZ's removed-at-the-yes histories). The 34 equal calls with a removed-only slot are inputs where that slot is in no offered evidence. The draft run (c01, before the last rows) gave the same split (FC12 16322 calls: 4 differ, all with a removed-only slot; FA03 1514/1514 equal). Per-process counts: iss\c02-fc12.11160.json, iss\c02-fa03.31680.json (the other file of each run is the node --test parent, 0 calls).

### 8. STOPs, disclosures and named debts

- STOPs: none. No template, reason, copy or displayed string changed (the FC01 hunk adds a helper and changes one expression; the FC03 hunks add checks, one constant and comments, change the S8 actual-load expression and the evidenceChanged comparison, and rebind PRODUCER_REVISION). The one ISSUANCE change is the authorized L23-B5 item, and section 5 shows every other issuance output byte-identical over the fixtures. No existing row went red on the final bytes (441/441); no existing row was edited or weakened (the round-31 R31-FUZZ count oracle, which admits "refused OR applied as genuine" for prefix/frontier, is unchanged: its refused branch is now the one taken, and the strict contract is pinned by R33-L23B2 and R33-FUZZ). Nothing outside FC01, FC03, FC12 and this report changed; FA03 unchanged (eb369300, 103). Nothing committed, staged or pushed; DECISIONS/STATUS untouched.
- D-R33-1 (Claude P1, spec ruling owed; not in the commission's words): S4 still prefers the slot's LIVE fact over a removed fact the record names, so a consumed slot re-logged with a NEW fact after a removal after the yes still refuses RECORD_INVALID evidence (consent lost). The commission names "a later removal of an added set and the undoing of that removal", which now keeps the consent; admitting a record that names a removed fact while another fact is live would also admit a record naming a fact removed BEFORE issuance, which :155 S4 ("original equal that authentic session's typed slot") does not settle.
- D-R33-2 (counts audit): typed now: load_basis.sets (round 32), load_basis.prefix, basis.order.frontier. Bound: evidence position (=== the typed slot's, S4). Left: load_basis.hi and tenure_start (the spec lists them at :119 without typing them as counts; hi is the programme's own value, keyed at :233 only when it is a positive safe integer; basisOf writes tenure_start null) and a FieldImage topRun value (a programme image, S8/(c1)-(c2)). Typing hi would refuse a genuine record over any programme whose hi is not a safe integer. A valid but different frontier or prefix is still applied (never compared by the fold; = D-R32F-2, spec-silent).
- D-R33-3 (multi-removed slot, spec-silent): a removed slot with SEVERAL removed facts has no single "its fact" (:109); issuance keeps original null there (the round-32 form), so undoing one of those removals after the yes still refuses. Own Q02/Q20 LIVE for that reason.
- D-R33-4 (no-fact state): a set naming no fact and no skip is bound to {unlogged, unresolved, removed}, not to one exact state: which of these the slot had at the issuance cut is not reconstructable from today's typed facts (an unlogged slot logged and removed after the yes reads removed today). No clause reads that state except evidenceChanged (repair notice only).
- D-R33-5 (carried K08 = D-R31C-3): where S4's proper-prefix replay is not faithful (an edit the typed fold cannot interpret), state is unbound exactly where current is; S8 now reads the bound current only.
- Disclosure (screen bytes): R33-ISSUANCE-SKIP-REF-KEPT (about 09:31) and R33-DR32F3-REPS-SAFE-COUNT (about 09:45) were appended to FC12 while the mutant screen (09:26-09:57) was running, and R33-STATE-OF-A-NAMED-REMOVED-FACT-AND-OF-NO-FACT after it; rows only gained rows (a kill stays a kill); every screened LIVE line was re-run whole on the final FC12, and the final whole FC12/FA03 runs are on the final bytes. The product bytes did not change during the screen.
- Disclosure (fuzz draft): my R33-FUZZ draft's count oracle first treated frontier 3 as a bad count (a01, 436/437); fixed in the draft before any claim (2 and 3 are bad only as the one-slot sets); its zero part was then balanced by owning clause (every clause reached at the default 1000 seeds), and the default raised from 600 to 1000 (the row runs in about 4 s).
- Disclosure (connection): the Desktop Commander bridge dropped once near the end (a sign-in prompt on one call, then it answered again); no run was affected (all runs had finished; the TAP hashing was simply re-run).
- Carried unchanged: D-R31C-3 (K08), D-R30C-5 (K10), D-R31C-4, D-R31C-5 and U's S1-S4, D-R32-3, D-R32-4, D-R32F-2, D-L23-RAW-DOMAIN, D-L23-FAITHFULNESS, D-L22-* and the earlier carried lists. O24 (D-R32C-3's member-order site in FC01 compensate()) is still killed only by R32-FUZZ (default seed); the commission listed the -0 sites, which R33-L23B3 now pins.

+++ b/rebuild/engine/native-load.cjs
@@ -131,9 +131,14 @@ function fieldImage(ex) {
 function baseLoad(ex) {
   return { scalar: loadOf(ex.w), vector: (ex.w == null ? Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, ex.sets || 1) }, () => null) : planVector(ex)).map(loadOf), fields: fieldImage(ex) };
 }
+// Round 33 (Astra L23-B5 = Claude D-R32C-1; DECISIONS:865 (3); spec :109 "original is its fact/skip Ref or null when absent; edits is
+// ordered [Ref]"): a slot whose ONE fact is removed (the typed projector keeps it among removed_facts, state removed) is recorded with
+// that fact's Ref and its edit Refs, so undoing the removal later is a later edit of the same fact (:155 D-R9-1, :166). Every other
+// slot is recorded exactly as before.
+const removedFact = (slot) => (!slot.fact && slot.state === 'removed' && Array.isArray(slot.removed_facts) && slot.removed_facts.length === 1 && map(slot.removed_facts[0]) ? slot.removed_facts[0] : null);
 function evidenceOf(rows, R) {
   return rows.map((row) => ({ start: R(row.start), close: R(row.close), sets: row.entry.slots.map((slot) => {
-    const f = slot.fact, cur = slot.state === 'performed' && f && f.current ? f.current : null;
+    const f = slot.fact || removedFact(slot), cur = slot.state === 'performed' && f && f.current ? f.current : null;
     return { slot: slot.logical_set_slot, position: slot.position, origin: slot.origin === undefined ? null : slot.origin, state: slot.state,
       original: f ? R(f.source_op_id) : text(slot.skip_op_id) ? R(slot.skip_op_id) : null,
       edits: (f && Array.isArray(f.edit_op_ids) ? f.edit_op_ids : []).map(R),
```

### 2. Product: the one fold change (FC03 sameCut; spec :155 ORIGINAL CUT and D-R9-1, :160; DECISIONS:867)

- Cause (as the reviewer located it): sameCut decides whether a present-revision record's ORIGINAL cut is reproduced ("exact fact-op coverage"). It checked coverage only for each slot's LIVE fact (and skip). A set removed after the yes moves its fact into `slot.removed_facts`; neither that fact nor its tombstone was checked, so the cut looked reproduced while the re-evaluation over today's slots (factsAtCut) saw the set removed and refused: RECORD_INVALID issuance, consent lost.
- Change: sameCut takes the record's responses (`yes` = the group's records, `[...g.ops, ...(g.alt || [])]`) and, per slot of the cut, reads every removed fact: an op of it (source op or any edit op, tombstone included) that is outside the record's coverage and NOT proven before any of its responses (provenBefore, the fold's one causal test) is a later edit, so the cut is not reproduced, exactly as a later correction of a live fact already is. Such a record goes to STRUCTURAL CORRESPONDENCE (S1-S8) and applies as written; its own BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED comes, as before, from evidenceChanged when its own consumed evidence changed. An uncovered removed-fact op that IS proven before the yes is ignored exactly as in round 33, so that record is re-evaluated and refused as before.
- Why order-aware and not the reviewer's probe (order-blind): the brief requires that a removal BEFORE the yes still refuses exactly as before. Measured (R34-M01 = the probe, mutant table): the order-blind form turns a yes recorded on an issuance that never saw an earlier removal (proven before the response, outside its coverage) from RECORD_INVALID issuance into applied. The order-aware form keeps that outcome byte-identical (R34-BR33C1-CONTROL part (a)).
- Nothing else: no FC01 byte, no issuance change, no template, reason, copy or displayed string, no revision move.

- SPEC OUTCOME, DISCLOSED (question Q-R34-1 for the PM): the brief and DECISIONS:867 say the yes is kept "with BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED". Measured on the three seeds: the removed sets lie OUTSIDE the consented earn's own evidence (another lift's set in a consumed session, or a same-lift set of an earlier, unconsumed session), so the earn now applies exactly as written with NO issue of its own, the same outcome FC03 already gives a later correction of an unconsumed fact (the existing comment at the reproduction branch: "a later correction of an unconsumed fact ... the body applies as written"). BASIS_REPAIR_REQUIRED is raised, as before, on the record whose OWN consumed set was removed (seed 33204 and the minimal history: the earlier adopt-observed, which then holds the lift, R9.2 :158). Raising REPAIR on a record whose evidence is unchanged would be a new rule (and would differ from the correction case); it is not done. The rows assert the outcome above.
